*Fang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出Riemannian Language Models (RiLM)，取消输出矩阵W_out，用状态与词表嵌入之间的平方测地距离产生下一词概率，在flat R^d（Flat RiLM）和Poincaré球（HypRiLM）两个实例上验证，HypRiLM在WikiText-2上取得54.2±0.2 PPL，优于对照组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小于一百万参数的语言模型对边缘部署、领域适配和可复现研究很重要，但即使在d=128时，两层LSTM或Transformer仍将约三分之一容量耗费在R^(d×|V|)的输出矩阵W_out上，造成参数浪费。作者的动机是彻底消除该输出层，以更高效的几何解码方式复用同一嵌入映射。

Method: 提出RiLM框架：将上下文展开为Riemannian流形上的轨迹，下一词概率由当前状态与词表嵌入之间的平方测地距离给出，输入与输出共享同一嵌入映射，解码即几何。实例化于平坦R^d（Flat RiLM）和Poincaré球H^d（HypRiLM），共享MLP组合映射phi（约290k参数，d=128，|V|=2000）。此外刻画了朴素双曲递归中的边界塌缩问题，并用Möbius稳定化恢复可训练性。

Result: 在WikiText-2上五个随机种子：HypRiLM达到54.2±0.2验证困惑度，Flat RiLM为87.6±0.6；tied与matched的LSTM、Transformer、SSM对照组维持在113–147 PPL，HypRiLM相对最强tied循环基线（SSM，113.0±3.8）领先约2倍。Penn Treebank与1万词表压力测试证实测地解码可跨语料和更大|V|迁移，双曲曲率具有选择性收益。

Conclusion: RiLM通过几何解码消除输出矩阵，在小模型受控对比中验证了测地解码的高效性与可迁移性，双曲曲率在特定条件下带来增益。作者强调结论限于小规模受控比较，并非全词表级别的SOTA声明。

Abstract: Language models under one million parameters matter for edge deployment, domain adaptation, and reproducible research, yet a two-layer LSTM or Transformer at embedding width d = 128 still spends roughly one third of its capacity on the output matrix W_out in R^(d x |V|). We propose Riemannian Language Models (RiLM), which remove that layer entirely: context unfolds as a trajectory on a Riemannian manifold, and next-token probabilities arise from squared geodesic distance between the current state and vocabulary embeddings. The same embedding map serves input and output -- decoding is geometry. We instantiate the framework on flat R^d (Flat RiLM) and the Poincare ball H^d (HypRiLM) with a shared MLP composition map phi (~290k parameters, d = 128, |V| = 2000). Across five seeds on WikiText-2, HypRiLM reaches 54.2 +/- 0.2 validation perplexity versus 87.6 +/- 0.6 for Flat RiLM; tied and matched LSTM, Transformer, and SSM controls remain at 113-147 PPL on WT-2 -- HypRiLM leads by roughly 2x over the strongest tied recurrent baseline (SSM, 113.0 +/- 3.8). Penn Treebank and a 10k-vocabulary stress test confirm that geodesic decoding transfers across corpora and larger |V|, while hyperbolic curvature helps selectively. We also characterize boundary collapse in naive hyperbolic recurrence and show how Mobius stabilization restores trainability. Claims are scoped to controlled small-model comparisons, not full-vocabulary state of the art.

</details>


### [141] [On-Policy Distillation for Vision-Language Model Adaptation, an Effective Paradigm on Low-Quality Multimodal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10321)
*Hongyuan Zhang,Xianda Guo,Yanlun Peng,Qianlong Yang,Yubin Guo,Pinhan Fu,Mulin Chen,Xiaozhen Qiao,Ping Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出OnPoKD，将视觉-语言蒸馏中的目标构建视为动态策略决策，学习轻量控制器为每个样本自适应构造蒸馏目标，在基类到新类泛化与跨数据集迁移基准上稳定优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言蒸馏方法通常以教师预测构建训练目标，并对所有训练样本统一应用，导致在类别偏移与域偏移下目标不可靠。作者主张蒸馏目标构建应是动态的训练决策，而非固定配方。

Method: OnPoKD是一个on-policy蒸馏框架：学习一个轻量控制器，利用来自教师模型、学生模型和零样本先验的可靠性线索与分歧线索，构造样本级自适应目标；控制器通过有界策略动作动态平衡教师监督、零样本先验引导和硬标签锚定；控制器以验证反馈更新，使目标构建优化可迁移性而非仅拟合训练分布。控制器仅在训练阶段使用，不改变推理结构与测试开销。

Result: 在Base-to-novel泛化与跨数据集迁移基准上，OnPoKD一致地优于强视觉-语言蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: 将蒸馏目标构建建模为可学习的策略决策是有效且实用的；OnPoKD可无缝集成到现有视觉-语言蒸馏流程中，在不增加推理成本的前提下提升迁移性能。据作者所知，这是首个将on-policy蒸馏应用于视觉-语言模型适配的框架。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation offers an efficient route to transfer a task-adapted vision-language teacher to a compact student. The training target in current vision-language distillation methods is typically constructed from the teacher prediction and applied uniformly to all training samples, making it unreliable under class and domain shifts. In this paper, we argue that distillation target construction should be treated as a dynamic training decision rather than a fixed recipe. To this end, we propose OnPoKD, an on-policy distillation framework for vision-language model adaptation. To the best of our knowledge, OnPoKD is the first framework that applies on-policy distillation to vision-language model adaptation by learning target construction as a policy decision. OnPoKD learns a lightweight controller that constructs sample-wise adaptive targets using reliability and disagreement cues from the teacher model, student model, and zero-shot prior. Instead of relying on a fixed teacher prediction, the controller dynamically balances teacher supervision, zero-shot prior guidance, and hard-label anchoring through bounded policy actions, allowing the distillation target to adapt to varying sample reliability and training stages. The policy controller is updated with validation feedback, encouraging target construction to optimize transferability rather than merely fitting the training distribution. Since the controller is only used during training, OnPoKD can be seamlessly integrated into existing vision-language distillation pipelines while preserving the original inference architecture and test-time cost. Extensive experiments on Base-to-novel generalization and Cross-dataset transfer benchmarks show that OnPoKD consistently improves over strong vision-language distillation baselines.

</details>


### [142] [Rosetta at AlexandriaX-2026: LoRA-Adapted NileChat for Context-Aware Dialectal Arabic Dialogue Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10395)
*Nada Esmaeil,Fathima Rena,Sibi Subhash,Osama Elgendy,Mina Naguib,Salma Omar,Muhammad Arif*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Rosetta 系统通过 LoRA 微调 NileChat-3B，结合方言与对话上下文的提示进行英译阿拉伯方言翻译，在受限赛道排名第 4，非受限赛道排名第 5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参与 AlexandriaX 共享任务中的子任务 1（上下文感知的英语到阿拉伯方言对话翻译），覆盖受限与非受限两个赛道。

Method: 在 NileChat-3B 上微调 LoRA 适配器，使用结构化 system/user 提示，将生成条件化于方言与对话上下文；非受限赛道中，额外在 MADAR 与 PADIC 上预训练该适配器。

Result: 受限赛道排名第 4（spBLEU 26.10），非受限赛道排名第 5（spBLEU 25.09）。实验表明外部预训练仅在 13 种方言中的 2 种上有效，同时略微拉低整体性能，提示存在负迁移。

Conclusion: 外部预训练带来的是负迁移，多数方言未受益且整体性能下降，说明额外的领域/方言预训练并非普遍有效。

Abstract: This paper describes the Rosetta system for Subtask 1 (Context-Aware English-to-Dialectal Arabic Dialogue Translation) of the AlexandriaX shared task, participating in both constrained and unconstrained tracks. The approach fine-tunes a LoRA adapter on NileChat-3B using structured system/user prompts that condition generation on dialect and dialogue context. For the unconstrained track, the adapter is additionally pretrained on MADAR and PADIC. Rosetta ranked 4th in the constrained track (spBLEU 26.10) and 5th in the unconstrained track (spBLEU 25.09). The experimental results demonstrate that external pretraining helps only two of thirteen dialects while slightly hurting overall performance, suggesting negative transfer.

</details>


### [143] [Can Foundation Models Moderate Online Content? Evaluating Instruction- vs. Example-Driven Policy Operationalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10410)
*Ayan Majumdar,Shounak Paul,Pushpdeep Singh,Ines Abdelaziz,Sayeh Jarollahi,Seungeon Lee,Krishna P. Gummadi,Ingmar Weber,Abhisek Dash*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含4000条Bluesky人工标注帖子的ModerationBench基准，系统比较了视觉语言模型（VLM）在内容审核中的两种指导范式——基于指令（从政策条文推理）与基于示例（从先例泛化）。结果显示基础模型可显著超越Bluesky现有审核系统，在Random Posts上F1分数近乎三倍（0.60 vs. 0.22），两种范式峰值效果相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内容审核政策日益复杂，其一致化落地运营面临关键挑战；基础模型虽具备应对该挑战的基本能力，但其能否可靠地审核在线内容仍未有定论。因此本文旨在系统探究VLM指导范式在该任务中的有效性与可靠性。

Method: 构建ModerationBench基准，包含来自Bluesky平台的4000条真实场景、人工标注帖子；在此基准上系统对比两种VLM指导范式：指令驱动（模型依据政策准则进行推理）与示例驱动（模型从既往判例中泛化）。

Result: 基础模型在ModerationBench上可大幅超越Bluesky已部署的审核系统，在Random Posts子集上F1分数提升近三倍（0.60 vs. 0.22）；指令驱动与示例驱动两种范式均能达到可比的峰值效果。

Conclusion: 基础模型（尤其VLM）具备实现可靠且可扩展的政策运营化潜力，为大规模、适应性强的内容审核政策落地提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The growing complexity of content moderation policies presents a critical challenge for their consistent operationalization. While foundation models possess the basic capabilities needed to confront this challenge, whether they can reliably moderate online content remains an unanswered question. In this paper, we systematically compare two competing paradigms for Vision-Language Model (VLM) guidance: an instruction-driven approach where models reason from policy precepts, and an example-driven approach where they generalize from prior precedents. We ground this investigation in ModerationBench, a new benchmark of 4,000 manually annotated, in-the-wild posts from the Bluesky platform. Our experiments reveal that foundation models can substantially outperform Bluesky's deployed moderation system, nearly tripling its $F_1$ score (0.60 vs. 0.22) on Random Posts in the benchmark, with both instruction- and example-driven paradigms achieving comparable peak effectiveness. Our findings thus chart a path toward reliable and adaptable policy operationalization at scale.

</details>


### [144] [Do speech foundation models really learn words?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10434)
*Robin Huo,Ewan Dunbar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过残差化剔除音素信息后，发现HuBERT与wav2vec 2.0的深层表示能独立于局部语音内容编码词，且该解耦方法可提升词发现任务中的高阶语言信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对自监督语音基础模型的评估多聚焦于其表示对音素和词的判别能力。然而，词级判别能力好并不必然意味着模型学到专门的词表示：对词的良好区分可能仅源于对词形（音素）的良好编码，而非编码了词身份或句法/语义属性的、与词形无关的表示。因此需要区分这两者。

Method: 采用残差化（residualization）方法，从自监督语音模型（HuBERT、wav2vec 2.0）的表示中偏出（partial out）音素信息，从而分离出独立于局部语音内容的词级表示，并检验其保真度及对下游任务的作用。

Result: 在模型的较深层中，HuBERT与wav2vec 2.0总体上确实学到了能独立于局部音素内容、并以合理保真度编码词的表示。这种简单的解耦方式能够增强词发现任务中的高阶语言信息。

Conclusion: 词级判别能力强不足以证明存在专门的词表示；通过残差化解耦音素信息后，可揭示自监督语音模型深层表示中所蕴含的、独立于词形的词级信息，并可用于提升词发现等任务。

Abstract: Self-supervised speech foundation models are now used in a wide array of downstream applications, including traditional speech recognition and as the basis for tokens in speech-aware language models. Attempts to understand their usefulness have largely focused on probing their representations' ability to discriminate phonemes and words. However, discriminative ability for words need not imply specialized representation of words per se. Good discrimination of words may be explained by good encoding of word form (phonemes) rather than form-independent word representations encoding identity or syntactic/semantic properties. By partialling out phoneme information using residualization, we show that, in later layers, HuBERT and wav2vec 2.0 do in general learn representations which encode words with reasonable fidelity independently of local phonetic content. We show that this simple approach to disentanglement can enhance higher-order linguistic information in word discovery tasks.

</details>


### [145] [Building Multilingual Bridges: Data Mixing as the Pillar of Generalization for In-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10445)
*Mehrnaz Mofakhami,Ananya Sahu,Alejandro R. Salamanca,Daniel D'souza,Alexandre Berard,Thomas Euyang,Marzieh Fadaee,Julia Kreutzer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 L2 reasoning 任务，通过优化 SFT 数据配比与调度，训练出 3.35B 的 Tiny Aya L2-Thinker，在 60 种语言、6 类基准上实现超 93% 的 L2 推理率，证明推理是可跨语言迁移的语言无关行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理语言模型高度英语中心化，无论用户以何种语言提问，模型主要以英语进行推理。这导致非英语用户使用受限、原问题意图可能丢失，且无法利用目标语言更易表达的知识，因此需要实现"用用户提示语言进行推理"（L2 reasoning）。

Method: 从数据出发，研究 SFT 中数据组成与调度策略对推理泛化的影响，构建 3.35B 规模的 Tiny Aya L2-Thinker 模型，并探究影响 L2 推理向未见语言泛化的关键因素。

Result: 在涵盖数学、常识推理、指令遵循、开放式生成与文化推理的 6 个基准、60 种语言上，L2 推理率超过 93%，同时保持较强整体性能。泛化到未见语言依赖三点：更广的语言覆盖、易获取的多语言非推理数据，以及足够的英语推理基座。

Conclusion: 推理是一种语言无关的行为，可通过精心设计的数据混合在类型学上多样化的语言间迁移，而无需为每种目标语言提供推理监督。作者开源模型权重与多语言推理数据以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Reasoning language models have made substantial advances on a variety of complex tasks, yet their capabilities remain overwhelmingly English-centric: models primarily reason in English regardless of the language they are prompted in. This is inaccessible for non-English-speaking users, risks losing the intent of the original question, and forgoes knowledge more readily expressed in the target language. In this work, we advance L2 reasoning, the ability of a model to reason consistently in the language of the user's prompt, thus building an in-language bridge between the prompt and the answer. We approach this problem from a data-centric angle, investigating how to optimize data composition and scheduling in SFT for reasoning generalization. Building Tiny Aya L2-Thinker at 3.35B scale, we achieve an L2 reasoning rate above 93% across 60 languages on 6 benchmarks spanning math, commonsense reasoning, instruction following, open-ended generation, and cultural reasoning while keeping performance strong. We show the path to generalizing L2 reasoning to held-out languages goes through broader language coverage, readily available multilingual non-reasoning data, and a sufficient English reasoning backbone. These findings indicate that reasoning is a language-agnostic behavior that can be transferred across typologically diverse languages through careful data mixing and without requiring reasoning supervision in every target language. We release our model weights and multilingual reasoning data to support further research on accessible, in-language reasoning.

</details>


### [146] [IBIB: A Protocol for Measuring Enterprise AI Systems by Serving Route, Not Model Identifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10494)
*Blake Stenstrom,Charangan Vasantharajan,Brian Sathianathan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出当前基准测试只对"广告版模型标识符"打分，忽略权重、服务路由、精度、输出契约与评测载体，导致可用能力测量存在系统性误差；提出IB2协议（绑定预检、含失败的一遍评分、结构性盲评仲裁）使该误差可报告，并以128个封印任务/987条断言的参考实例给出四项实证结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业部署的是"系统"而非"检查点"，可用能力由权重、服务路由、精度、输出契约与评测载体共同决定，但现有18个受审基准均只对宣传的模型标识符打分，这一错位构成测量误差，且该误差此前不可报告。

Method: 提出IB2协议，包含三部分：金标准盲的"能力绑定预检"（在任务送达前验证路由能否执行评测契约）；"可靠性纳入式"首遍评分规则（保留失败计入分数，同时排除未获支持的能力）；结构性盲评的仲裁机制。并发布算法、分类表、请求契约与清单模式。

Result: 以128个封印任务、987条断言（覆盖文档、表格、图表、工具与数据库工作）的参考实例，在11个系统上得到四点：能力可用性可测（同一权重两次完整单路由运行后分别未通过最终绑定门的不同谓词，第三次在全新运行前通过，而宣传标识符未暴露任何限制）；区分度不均（7个套件中4个在六系统带宽内饱和，差异几乎全部来自受治理的数据库工作与多标签页连接，故报告区间支持的"分辨率分组"而非排名；标称五标签输出的四个切点中两个未通过多重性校正）；服务臂选择使某一声明修订与精度从77.38变为82.54，配对区间[0.11,10.60]，但各臂在访问模式、载体代际与服务工具调用解析器上不同，且载体代际属评测器属性而非端点属性；将失败响应排除出分母会改变点序，故"可靠性纳入"改变的是结论而非措辞。

Conclusion: 基准应把"系统级可用能力"作为测量对象，IB2将路由/契约/可靠性纳入评分并使误差可报告；报告应采用区间支持的分辨率分组而非排名，且须披露服务臂、载体代际与失败计入方式，因为它们会实质改变结论。过程（协议与模式）即交付物，语料保持封印。

Abstract: Enterprises deploy systems, not checkpoints. Usable capability depends jointly on weights, serving route, precision, output contract, and harness, yet all 18 audited benchmarks score advertised model identifiers. We treat this as measurement error and give a protocol that makes it reportable. It has three parts. A gold-blind capability-binding preflight verifies that a route can execute the evaluation contract before any task reaches it; a reliability-inclusive first-pass scoring rule keeps failure in the score while keeping unsupported capability out; and adjudication is structurally score-blind. We call the protocol IB2 and release its algorithms, classification tables, request contract, and manifest schemas. Its reference instantiation, 128 locked tasks and 987 assertions over document, spreadsheet, chart, tool and database work, stays sealed: the procedure is the artifact, not the corpus. Across eleven systems, four results. Capability availability is measurable: two complete single-route runs on identical weights later failed distinct predicates of the finalized binding gate, while a third passed that gate before a fresh run. The advertised identifier exposed neither limit. Discrimination is not uniform: four of seven suites saturate under a six-system band, with the spread almost entirely from governed database work and multi-tab joins, so we report interval-backed resolution groups, not ranks; two of the nominal five-label output's four cuts fail multiplicity adjustment. Serving-arm choice moved one declared revision and precision from 77.38 to 82.54, paired interval [0.11,10.60], though the arms differ in access mode, harness generation, and the serving tool-call parser, and harness generation is a property of our evaluator, not any endpoint. Excluding failed responses from denominators changes the point ordering, so reliability inclusion changes a conclusion, not its wording.

</details>


### [147] [IdeaAMBIG: Benchmarking Implementation-Critical Gaps in Research-Idea Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10539)
*Yiling Ma,Yilun Zhao,Sihong Wu,Manasi Patwardhan,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出IdeaAMBIG基准，用于评估研究方法规范的"可编码就绪性"，涵盖三种能力：就绪性评估、缺陷定位、澄清动作生成；结果显示缺陷定位是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究想法可能新颖、连贯且科学上合理，但其提出的方法规范往往不足以支持忠实实现。作者关注"可编码就绪性"问题：即规范是否提供足够的方法信息，使有能力的实现者或编码智能体能在无需无依据假设的情况下构建预期方法。

Method: 构建基于证据的规范及其受支持解决方案，来源包括论文、代码库、issue讨论和复现工件。提出IdeaAMBIG基准，包含660个实例：163个来自可复现性报告和GitHub issue的真实缺口，497个注入到可编码就绪参考中的受控合成缺口。评估三种能力：可编码就绪性评估、缺陷定位、澄清动作生成。其中缺陷定位仅接收规范，澄清还额外接收标注缺陷。

Result: 在13个LLM上测试，最佳模型在真实实例上取得9.6%的宏缺陷回收率，但在给定缺陷时宏澄清动作成功率达80.6%。在oracle研究中，提供gold resolution使下游可编码就绪率从14%提升至98%。所有模型中缺陷定位都是主要瓶颈，给定缺陷后澄清表现更强。

Conclusion: 研究方法规范的可编码就绪性评估中，缺陷定位是核心瓶颈；若缺陷已知，澄清能力显著更强，gold resolution可将可编码就绪率大幅提升至98%，表明定位缺口是实现落地的关键挑战。

Abstract: A research idea may be novel, coherent, and scientifically plausible, yet its proposed method may remain insufficiently specified for faithful implementation. We study the codification readiness of implementation-facing research-method specifications, defined by whether they provide sufficient methodological information for a competent implementer or coding agent to construct the intended method without unsupported assumptions. We construct evidence-grounded specifications and their supported resolutions from papers, codebases, issue threads, and reproduction artifacts. We introduce IdeaAMBIG, a benchmark of 660 evidence-grounded instances: 163 real-world gaps from reproducibility reports and GitHub issues, and 497 controlled synthetic gaps injected into codification-ready references. IdeaAMBIG evaluates three capabilities: codification-readiness assessment, defect localization, and clarification action generation. Defect localization receives only the specification, whereas clarification additionally receives the annotated defect. Across 13 LLMs, the best model achieves 9.6% Macro Defect Recovery Rate on real-world instances but 80.6% Macro Clarification Action Success Rate when given the defect. In an oracle study, supplying the gold resolution raises the downstream codification-ready rate from 14% to 98%. Across all evaluated models, defect localization is the main bottleneck, with stronger clarification given the defect.

</details>


### [148] [Every Activation Boosted: Scaling General Reasoner to 1 Trillion Open Language Foundation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22115)
*Ling Team,Ang Li,Ben Liu,Binbin Hu,Bing Li,Bingwei Zeng,Borui Ye,Caizhi Tang,Changxin Tian,Chao Huang,Chao Zhang,Chen Qian,Chenchen Ju,Chenchen Li,Chengfu Tang,Chilin Fu,Chunshao Ren,Chunwei Wu,Cong Zhang,Cunyin Peng,Dafeng Xu,Daixin Wang,Dalong Zhang,Dingnan Jin,Dingyuan Zhu,Dongke Hu,Fangzheng Zhao,Feifan Wu,Feng Zhu,Gangshan Wang,Haitao Zhang,Hailin Zhao,Hanxiao Zhang,Hanzi Wang,Hao Qian,Haoyi Yu,Heng Zhang,Hongliang Zhang,Hongzhi Luan,Huirong Dong,Huizhong Li,Jia Li,Jia Liu,Jialong Zhu,Jian Sha,Jianping Wei,Jiaolong Yang,Jieyue Ma,Jiewei Wu,Jinjing Huang,Jingyun Tian,Jingyuan Zhang,Jinquan Sun,Juanhui Tu,Jun Liu,Jun Xu,Jun Zhou,Junjie Ou,Junpeng Fang,Kaihong Zhang,Kaiqin Hu,Ke Shi,Kun Tang,Kunlong Chen,Lanyin Mei,Lei Liang,Lei Xu,Libo Zhang,Lin Ju,Lin Yuan,Ling Zhong,Lintao Ma,Lu Liu,Lu Yu,Lun Cai,Meiqi Zhu,Mengying Li,Min Chen,Minghao Xue,Minghong Cai,Mingming Yin,Peijie Jiang,Peilong Zhao,Pingping Liu,Qian Zhao,Qing Cui,Qingxiang Huang,Qingyuan Yang,Quankun Yu,Shaowei Wei,Shijie Lian,Shoujian Zheng,Shun Song,Shungen Zhang,Shuo Zhang,Siyuan Li,Song Liu,Ting Guo,Tong Zhao,Wanli Gu,Weichang Wu,Weiguang Han,Wenjing Fang,Wubin Wang,Xiang Shu,Xiao Shi,Xiaoshun Lan,Xiaolu Zhang,Xiaqing Sun,Xin Zhao,Xingyu Lu,Xiong Xu,Xudong Wang,Xudong Wang,Xuemin Yang,Yajie Yang,Yang Xiang,Yanzhe Li,Yi Zhang,Yilong Wang,Yingxue Li,Yongzhen Guo,Yuzhuo Fu,Yuanyuan Wang,Yue Yang,Yue Yu,Yufeng Deng,Yun Zhang,Yunfei Yu,Yuqi Zhang,Yuxiao He,Zengke Gui,Zhaoxin Huan,Zhaoyang Wang,Zhibo Zhu,Zhihao Wang,Zhiqiang Zhang,Zhoufei Wang,Zihang Zeng,Ziqi Liu,Zitao Xuan,Zuoli Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Ling 2.0 是一系列面向推理的语言基础模型，基于统一 MoE 范式，覆盖16B至1T总参数，通过高稀疏、跨尺度一致性与效率优化，在万亿规模实现推理精度与计算效率的新帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建可扩展且高效的推理导向语言基础模型，探索稀疏激活与推理目标的正确对齐，从而以更低激活计算量获得更强推理能力，并为后续推理/思考模型提供统一开放基座。

Method: 采用统一MoE范式与高稀疏设计，引入MTP提升高效推理；进行推理导向数据构建与中训练CoT激活；采用基于强化的微调方法（DFT、Evo-CoT）；实现全尺度FP8训练与细粒度异构流水线；覆盖架构、预训练、后训练与基础设施的协同创新。

Result: 发布三个非思考（instruct）模型：Ling-mini-2.0、Ling-flash-2.0、Ling-1T，总参数量从16B到1T；相比稠密对应模型实现最高约7倍的激活计算效率；Ling-1T在万亿规模上建立了推理精度与计算效率的新帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: Ling 2.0 表明，当稀疏激活与推理目标良好对齐时，可实现可扩展且高效智能，为未来推理与思考模型（包括基于同一基座的Ring系列）提供连贯、开放、高效的基础。

Abstract: We introduce Ling 2.0, a series reasoning-oriented language foundation built upon the principle that every activation boosts reasoning capability. Designed to scale from tens of billions to one trillion parameters under a unified Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) paradigm, Ling 2.0 emphasizes high sparsity, cross-scale consistency, and efficiency guided by empirical scaling laws. The series includes three non-thinking (instruct) models - Ling-mini-2.0, Ling-flash-2.0, and Ling-1T - ranging from 16B to 1T total parameters and achieving up to 7-fold active-compute efficiency compared with dense counterparts. Ling 2.0 integrates coordinated innovations across model architecture, pre-training, post-training, and infrastructure: a high-sparsity MoE with MTP for efficient reasoning, reasoning-oriented data and mid-training CoT activation, reinforcement-based fine-tuning (DFT, Evo-CoT), and full-scale FP8 training with fine-grained heterogeneous pipelines. At the trillion scale, Ling-1T establishes a new Pareto frontier of reasoning accuracy versus computational efficiency, demonstrating that sparse activation, when properly aligned with reasoning objectives, enables scalable and efficient intelligence. Collectively, Ling 2.0 provides a coherent, open, and efficient foundation for advancing future reasoning and thinking models, including the Ring series built upon the same base.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [OpenDiscoveryTrace: Process Traces for Evaluating AI Scientist Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09203)
*Aayam Bansal,Keertan Balaji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出并公开了 OpenDiscoveryTrace 数据集，包含 558 条完整 AI 科学智能体推理轨迹，记录其推理过程而非仅最终产出，用于过程级评估、失败模式诊断与科学智能体审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主 AI 科学家基准仅评估最终产出（生成的代码、假设或论文），丢弃了获得这些产出所依赖的推理过程，导致无法审计科学方法论、诊断失败模式，也无法区分系统性推理与侥幸猜测。

Method: 构建公开数据集 OpenDiscoveryTrace，每条轨迹按每步 9 字段结构化记录（思维、工具调用、观察、错误、修订触发、自报置信度等）；覆盖 124 项科学任务（药物发现、材料科学、基因组学、科学文献分析）；涵盖七个模型：三个前沿模型（GPT-5.4、Claude Opus 4.6、Gemini 3.1 Pro，各 124 条，按领域与难度完全平衡）与四个开放权重模型（Qwen2.5-7B、Mistral-7B-v0.3、Phi-3.5-mini、Qwen2.5-1.5B，各 30 条），外加 60 条实时检索变体轨迹；定义五项基准任务并给出逻辑回归、随机森林、LSTM 与 Transformer 基线。

Result: 对 363 条 LLM 评判轨迹的试点分析显示，过程轨迹可揭示仅看产出无法发现的行为差异：三个前沿模型成功率相近（84–89%），但 Claude Opus 4.6 的每轨迹错误数约为 GPT-5.4 的 30 倍（2.5 对 0.08，p < 0.0001，Cliff's δ = 0.613），且错误画像质上不同——Claude 中 66.7% 为工具误用，GPT-5.4 中 83.6% 为推理错误。

Conclusion: 数据集、轨迹模式、智能体运行框架与基准定义均以 CC BY 4.0 公开，以支持过程级评估、科学智能体审计与 AI 治理研究。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for autonomous AI scientists evaluate only final outputs---generated code, hypotheses, or papers---yet discard the reasoning process by which those outputs were obtained. This makes it impossible to audit scientific methodology, diagnose failure modes, or distinguish systematic reasoning from fortunate guessing. We present \textbf{OpenDiscoveryTrace}, a public dataset of 558 complete AI scientific agent trajectories that captures how models reason, not just what they produce. Each trajectory records a structured 9-field-per-step trace---including thoughts, tool calls, observations, errors, revision triggers, and self-reported confidence---as models execute 124 scientific tasks spanning drug discovery, materials science, genomics, and scientific literature analysis. The dataset covers seven models: three frontier models (GPT-5.4, Claude Opus 4.6, and Gemini 3.1 Pro; 124 trajectories each, fully balanced across domains and difficulty levels) and four open-weight models (Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B-v0.3, Phi-3.5-mini, and Qwen2.5-1.5B; 30 each), plus 60 live-retrieval variant trajectories. Pilot analysis on 363 LLM-judged trajectories reveals that process traces expose behavioral differences invisible to output-only evaluation: all three frontier models achieve comparable success rates (84--89%), yet Claude Opus 4.6 produces 30$\times$ more errors than GPT-5.4 (2.5 vs. 0.08 per trajectory, $p < 0.0001$, Cliff's $δ= 0.613$), with qualitatively different error profiles---66.7% tool misuse for Claude versus 83.6% reasoning errors for GPT-5.4. We define five benchmark tasks with baselines from logistic regression, random forests, LSTMs, and Transformer models. The dataset, trace schema, agent harness, and benchmark definitions are publicly available under CC BY 4.0 to support research on process-level evaluation, scientific agent auditing, and AI governance.

</details>


### [150] [Adaptive Entangled Game Modules in Artificial General Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09226)
*Haochen Li,Xinshuai Guo,Jingdong Ouyang,Wei Zhang,Leilei Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出概率波框架与广义行为智能（GBI）非局域概率波方程，用中国日内股市数据检验LCA非局域纠缠神经纤维假说，发现纠缠博弈模式解释了89%的决策行为，并主张将纠缠博弈模块引入AGI架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望为相互作用的自适应主体集体行为建立可检验的建模框架，并借此间接检验Liu-Chen-Ao（LCA）关于大脑非局域纠缠神经纤维的假说，同时指出依赖海量不透明参数的传统ANN式AI的局限，为AGI架构寻找更类脑的机制。

Method: 提出概率波框架，通过广义行为智能（GBI）非局域概率波方程推导可检验的本征模式；以中国股市日内高频数据为实证对象，用自适应纠缠博弈模式与独立理性主体模式（新古典金融预测）进行对比拟合。

Result: 自适应纠缠博弈模式解释82–94%（总体89%）的观测决策模式，与基于独立理性主体的新古典金融预测形成鲜明对比；2–12%的行为表现为对日内新闻、事件与环境的适应，具有双均衡态与参考点突变特征；纯独立模式占比不足5%。

Conclusion: 实证结果支持LCA假说，即可观测交易行为反映底层脑机制与行为心理学中的内部智能决策；作者主张在AGI架构中引入自适应纠缠博弈模块，将ANN式AI与基于概率波的纠缠脑模拟结合，以丰富AGI基础模型并推动类脑处理单元（HPU）发展，从而构建更紧凑、高效、鲁棒的AGI系统，尤其面向具身智能与机器人。

Abstract: We introduce a probability-wave framework for modeling the collective behavior of interacting adaptive agents, deriving testable eigenmodes through a generalized behavioral intelligence (GBI) nonlocal probability-wave equation. This framework captures a broad range of human intelligence behaviors with analytical mechanisms and offers an indirect method to examine the Liu-Chen-Ao (LCA) hypothesis of nonlocal entangled nerve fibers in the brain through collective trader behaviors. Our empirical analysis of Chinese intraday stock market data demonstrates that adaptive entangled game modes explain 82-94% (89% overall) of observed decision patterns, a sharp contrast to the predictions of neoclassical finance based on independent rational agents. Moreover, 2-12% of behaviors show adaption to intraday news, events, and environments, characterized by dual equilibrium states and abrupt reference point shifts, while purely independent modes occur in less than 5% of cases. These findings empirically support the LCA hypothesis, as observable trading behaviors reflect underlying brain mechanisms and internal intelligence decision-making in behavioral psychology. Our results highlight the necessity of incorporating adaptive entangled game modules into artificial general intelligence (AGI) architectures, addressing the limitations of conventional artificial neural network (ANN)-based AI, which relies on trillions of opaque parameters. By integrating ANN-based AI with probability-wave-based entangled-brain simulations, machine learning can enrich AGI foundation models (FMs) and facilitate the development of human-like processing units (HPUs) that leverage brain-inspired mechanisms. Such HPUs may ultimately create more compact, efficient, and robust AGI systems, particularly for embodied intelligence and robotics.

</details>


### [151] [Subagents vs Agent Skills: Executing Reusable Knowledge for Long-Horizon Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09233)
*Wasu Top Piriyakulkij,Rachel Lawrence,Alicia Curth,Sushrut Karmalkar,Niranjani Prasad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型智能体如何有效利用可复用知识库解决长时程任务，比较了"技能包作为上下文指令加载"与"技能包作为子智能体调用"两种执行方式，发现后者在技能包具备清晰输入-输出契约时表现更优，代价是额外的通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着任务时程增长，将技能指令直接加载进主上下文的方式变得脆弱：上下文窗口内信息累积会降低推理质量。因此需要探究一种替代方案，使可复用知识在高负载、长时程场景下仍能稳定发挥作用。

Method: 提出并对比两种技能执行范式：(1) agent-skill execution——把技能包（包含指令、脚本、资源的多文件包）的指令加载进智能体上下文并依赖其遵循；(2) subagent execution——将技能包作为子智能体调用，为每个子任务生成全新的独立上下文窗口，不污染主上下文，但需在主智能体与子智能体之间进行协调通信。

Result: 当技能包暴露出清晰的输入-输出契约，且其指令编码了履行该契约所需的程序性知识时，子智能体执行优于技能-上下文执行。其代价是额外的通信开销，即主智能体与子智能体协调需要消耗额外 token。

Conclusion: 可复用知识的收益不仅取决于知识内容本身，还取决于其组织方式与调用方式；将技能包作为子智能体调用是缓解长上下文推理退化的一种有效策略，但需以清晰的接口契约与额外的通信成本为条件。

Abstract: How can language model agents effectively leverage libraries of reusable knowledge to solve long-horizon tasks? Recent work has increasingly focused on agent skills: reusable capabilities represented as skill packages, i.e., multi-file bundles containing instructions, scripts, and other resources that help agents perform specific tasks. Agent skills are typically executed by loading their skill instructions into an agent's context and relying on the agent to follow them. As task horizons grow, however, this approach becomes increasingly brittle, because reasoning quality degrades as more information accumulates in the context window. We investigate an alternative approach in which skill packages are instead invoked as subagents. Rather than loading skill instructions into the main context, subagent execution spawns fresh context windows dedicated to solving individual subtasks. We show that subagent execution outperforms agent-skill execution when skill packages expose clear input-output contracts and their instructions encode the procedural knowledge needed to fulfill those contracts. The tradeoff is additional communication overhead, as extra tokens are required to coordinate between the main agent and its subagents. Our results show that the benefit of reusable knowledge depends not only on its content, but also on how it is organized and invoked.

</details>


### [152] [Gradland: On Phenomenal Experience, Differentiated Across Many Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09306)
*David Balduzzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在理想化的"Gradland"世界中检验"一阶结构（梯度/雅可比）刻画现象经验结构"的假设，提出有效秩与凝聚度两个雅可比结构度量，并用系列实例说明该假设可解释7类经验现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文试图建立物理交互的一阶结构（梯度或雅可比）与现象经验结构之间的联系，探索经验结构是否可由可微神经网络的雅可比性质来刻画。

Method: 构建理想化世界Gradland，其中物理规律已知、函数（大多）可微，主体为神经网络；基于Kirchhoff复杂度提出两种雅可比结构度量：有效秩（effective rank）与凝聚度（cohesion）；通过在Gradland中的一系列算例进行验证。

Result: 该假设在所给算例中成功解释了7种现象：(1)经验可延续数百毫秒的持续时间；(2)鲜明与模糊经验之别；(3)质地/纹理经验；(4)新生儿"繁茂喧闹的混乱"；(5)清晰持存于心的观念与混乱观念之别；(6)学习过程的体验；(7)丰富密集经验的功能。

Conclusion: 在Gradland的理想化框架下，以雅可比的一阶结构（有效秩、凝聚度）刻画现象经验结构具有一定解释力，可为经验的时间延展、清晰度差异、纹理、婴儿混乱、观念清晰性、学习体验及丰富经验的功能提供统一说明。

Abstract: This paper investigates the hypothesis that the first-order structure of physical interactions, i.e. gradients or Jacobians, characterizes the structure of phenomenal experience. It does so in an idealized world inhabited by neural networks, Gradland, where the physics are known and the functions are (mostly) differentiable. The paper introduces two measures of Jacobian structure: effective rank and cohesion, based on Kirchhoff complexity. Applying the measures to a series of worked examples shows the hypothesis accounts for: (1) the duration of experience, that it can prolong over hundreds of milliseconds; (2) the difference between what is experienced vividly and obscurely; (3) the experience of texture; (4) the blooming buzzing confusion presumably experienced by newborns; (5) the difference between ideas that are held distinctly in mind and ideas that are confused; (6) what learning is like; and finally (7) the paper explains the function of rich, dense experience.

</details>


### [153] [An Autonomous GeoAI Agent for Arctic Eco-Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09374)
*Samira Alkaee Taleghan,Younghyun Koo,Farnoush Banaei-Kashani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个人类参与（human-in-the-loop）、多智能体（multi-agent）的GeoAI系统，用于北极生态导航（eco-navigation），将运营、物理、生态与社区多准则统一于航线规划框架中，支持生成帕累托天际线并交由人类做价值判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 北极海冰变化使季节性通航性提升，但带来运营、环境与社区风险。北极航线规划本质上是多准则问题：提升船舶安全或效率的航线可能加剧对海冰、敏感生态系统及邻近社区的暴露。现有航线方法偏重航行时间、燃油消耗与航行风险，常忽视生态与社区影响，因此需要一个能兼顾生态与社区维度的透明决策框架。

Method: 构建人类参与的多智能体GeoAI系统。多个专业化智能体协同完成：地理空间数据的获取与预处理、多目标航线生成，以及基于天际线（skyline）的决策支持。生态准则显式建模对敏感区域的暴露，包括重要鱼类栖息地（Essential Fish Habitat）与海豹关键栖息地（seal critical habitat）；同时纳入社区相关负担考量，并将关键价值权衡交由人类控制。

Result: 摘要为框架性介绍，未给出定量实验指标；系统实现了将运营、物理、生态与社区准则整合进统一航线规划流程，并提供多目标候选航线与天际线级决策支持，项目页面与代码已公开。

Conclusion: 在保留人类对价值判断主导权的前提下，该框架可支持更安全、更透明且更具社会责任的北极航行决策，弥补现有方法在生态与社区影响方面的缺失。

Abstract: Arctic maritime navigation is becoming increasingly important as changing sea-ice conditions expand seasonal accessibility while simultaneously introducing substantial operational, environmental, and community risks. Arctic route planning is inherently a multi-criteria problem: routes that improve vessel safety or efficiency may increase exposure to sea ice, sensitive ecosystems, or nearby communities. Existing routing methods prioritize travel time, fuel use, and navigational risk, often overlooking ecological and community impacts. We introduce a human-in-the-loop, multi-agent GeoAI system for Arctic eco-navigation that integrates operational, physical, ecological, and community-related criteria within a unified routing framework. Multiple specialized agents coordinate geospatial data acquisition and preparation, multi-objective route generation, and skyline-based decision support. The ecological criteria explicitly account for exposure to sensitive areas, including Essential Fish Habitat and seal critical habitat. By considering these ecosystem impacts and potential community burdens while keeping consequential value judgments under human control, the framework supports safer, more transparent, and socially responsible Arctic navigation. Project page and code are publicly available. https://samiraat.github.io/Arctic-Eco-Navigation-Agent/, https://github.com/samiraat/Arctic-Eco-Navigation-Agent

</details>


### [154] [The Menu Is an Execution Prior: State-Path Tool Menus for Online Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09395)
*Bo Yan,Weikai Lin,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"状态路径工具菜单"（State-Path Tool Menu），用执行先验的方式学习从请求状态到目标结果的工具调用路线，在ToolBench上将在线成功率从0.737提升至0.898。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际智能体面对包含数千个接口的工具库，而执行前只能看到一个简短的、有顺序的工具子集（即工具菜单）。多步任务不仅需要最终动作，还需要能生成其输入的前置工具，且需按可用顺序排列。现有构造器仅按请求相关性对工具排序，容易显露最终动作，却遗漏或延迟那些不显眼的生产者工具。

Method: 1) 提出"状态路径"概念，即从可观测的请求状态到目标结果的执行前路线；2) 提出State-Path Tool Menu框架，将菜单建模为这些路线上的执行先验；3) 编码器表示：当前状态下哪些工具可运行、其输出如何满足后续输入、训练路径中哪些顺序反复出现；4) 检索器覆盖可执行入口、缺失输入的生成器及最终动作；5) 重排序器将生产者置于消费者之前。

Result: 在ToolBench上，该菜单将在线成功率从0.737提升至0.898，优于检索、重排序、生成和路由等基线，且无需改动智能体本身。其32个工具即可覆盖比官方128个工具更完整的调用链，且成功增益在不同模型容量的执行器家族间保持稳定。

Conclusion: 按状态路径组织工具菜单可有效解决相关性排序导致的前置工具缺失问题，在更少工具数量下覆盖更完整的执行链，并具备跨执行器的泛化能力。

Abstract: Language models act through tools, yet practical agents face libraries containing thousands of interfaces. We introduce the tool menu as the short, ordered subset of available tools shown to an agent before execution. The agent can call only tools in this menu. Multi-step tasks require the final action and the prerequisite tools that create its inputs in a usable order. Current constructors rank tools by request relevance, which can surface the final action while omitting or delaying less obvious producers. We introduce the state path, a pre-execution route from the observable request state to the desired outcome, and propose State-Path Tool Menu to learn it. Our framework treats the menu as an execution prior over these routes. Its encoder represents which tools can run from the current state, how their outputs satisfy later inputs, and which orders recur in training paths. A retriever covers an executable entry, the missing-input producers, and the final action. A reranker then places producers before consumers. On ToolBench, our menu raises online success from 0.737 to 0.898 and outperforms retrieval, reranking, generation, and routing baselines without changing the agent. The State-Path menu also covers more complete chains with 32 tools than the official list covers with 128, and its success gain persists across executor families with different model capacities. Our code is at https://github.com/Met2348/State-Path.

</details>


### [155] [Decision-Focused Active Learning for Scale-Aware Critical-Materials Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09413)
*Niranjan Srinivas,Debajyoti Ray,Elias Nakouzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究基于CICERO自主选择性沉淀流程记录，用条件回顾性基准证明主动学习比非自适应空间填充更少实验即可找到最优结果，并提出以"下游贝叶斯风险期望下降"作为批次选择准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放大回收工艺的选型需将实验室结果与产品要求、工艺成本和规模效应相连接，而传统实验设计（如空间填充）效率低、缺乏与决策成本的耦合。作者希望用已有记录评估主动学习的实际收益，并建立可直接对接决策的批次选择原则。

Method: 以太平洋西北国家实验室CICERO（面向关键元素回收与优化的计算机智能）自主选择性沉淀工作流记录为数据基础；主动学习利用先前结果选择实验；在条件回顾性基准中使用拟合模型与再生NdFeB磁体记录，将主动学习与非自适应空间填充对比；以富集度（稀土与铁比例相对进料）为目标；对再生SmCo磁体做条件分析；并提出按"当前信念下可用工艺决策的最小期望损失（下游贝叶斯风险的期望下降）"选择批次，进行探索性模拟比较过滤候选的混合策略与已实施的跨路线-条件联合搜索。

Result: 主动学习以16–24孔（单次实验）达到记录的最高富集度，而非自适应方法需48孔；两阶段重构策略与两种自适应方案在16孔处持平。SmCo再生磁体的条件分析显示第2轮存在纯度与名义收率（基于假定起始量计算的回收分数）的权衡；NdFeB第1轮各路线在富集度上存在差异。油气开采产出水的排序依赖于相态与稀释假设，尚需确认。探索性模拟中，带候选过滤的混合策略估计损失低于已实施的联合搜索；涉及合成两阶段策略的差异相对估计不确定性较小。

Conclusion: 主动学习在回顾性基准中显著减少达到最优结果所需实验数，下游贝叶斯风险准则为批次选择提供了与决策成本一致的框架；但两阶段策略间差异在估计不确定性范围内，需通过共享损失与日志标准的预注册前瞻性试验验证，并要求测量与记录澄清、明确的工艺决策与相关输出、可信的经济输入以及目标规模验证。

Abstract: Choosing a recovery process for scale-up requires connecting laboratory results with product requirements, process costs, and scale effects. We analyze records from Pacific Northwest National Laboratory's Computer Intelligence for Critical Element Recovery and Optimization (CICERO) workflow for autonomous selective precipitation. Active learning uses prior results to choose experiments. In a conditional retrospective benchmark with fitted models and recycled neodymium-iron-boron (NdFeB) magnet records, active learning finds the best recorded result with fewer experiments than nonadaptive space filling. Enrichment is the selected rare-earth-to-iron ratio relative to that in the feed. Adaptive policies reach the recorded enrichment maximum by 16 to 24 wells (individual experiments), versus 48. Our two-stage reconstruction ties two adaptive alternatives at 16 wells. Conditional analyses of recycled samarium-cobalt (SmCo) magnets show a Round 2 tradeoff between purity and nominal yield, the recovery fraction calculated from an assumed starting amount - NdFeB Round 1 routes differ in enrichment. Rankings for produced water from oil and gas extraction depend on phase and dilution assumptions requiring confirmation.
  We propose choosing batches by their expected reduction in downstream Bayes risk: the minimum expected loss among available process decisions under current beliefs. In exploratory simulations, a hybrid that filters candidates has lower estimated loss than the implemented joint search across routes and conditions. Differences involving the synthetic two-stage policy are small relative to estimation uncertainty. We outline a pre-registered prospective test under a shared loss and logging standard, requiring clarified measurements and records, a defined process decision and relevant outputs, credible economic inputs, and validation at the intended scale.

</details>


### [156] [Valerant: An Automatic Navigable Game Map Generator via Action-Conditioned World Model Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09418)
*Yiran Qiao,Feng Wang,Jing Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架Valerant，将预训练动作条件世界模型转化为世界动作模型（WAM），通过预测性视觉rollout、SLAM空间重建与探索驱动动作选择，将单张图像逐步转化为持久可导航的3D游戏地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）在具身智能中发展迅速，但游戏领域的通用方案仍属空白。现有游戏方法多将动作条件世界模型与外部策略、奖励函数结合，主要在2D视觉观测空间运行，未构建持久3D几何。将WAM范式扩展到3D游戏面临独特挑战：自动驾驶与机器人中物理环境独立于模型存在，提供可执行动作的持久3D世界，而游戏没有这种外部基底，虚拟世界必须由模型自身实例化；3D游戏还需支持移动与交互的显式几何，而动作条件视频rollout只提供视觉观测、不提供空间表征。

Method: Valerant为免训练框架，基于预训练的动作条件世界模型。其核心是将预测性视觉rollout与基于SLAM的空间重建相耦合，并结合探索驱动的动作选择，从单张图像出发，逐步实例化出持久、可导航的3D游戏地图。

Result: Valerant能够从单张图像渐进构建出持久3D游戏地图，将WAM式交互从2D视觉模拟扩展到具备显式几何的3D空间，并可用于降低3D游戏地图制作的人工成本。

Conclusion: 该工作将WAM范式从2D视觉模拟拓展到3D游戏空间的探索与构建，为减少3D游戏地图制作的人工投入提供了新思路。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) couple predictive world modeling with action generation, allowing anticipated future states to guide agent behavior. Although WAMs are rapidly advancing embodied AI, general-purpose counterparts remain largely unexplored in games. Existing game-oriented approaches often combine action-conditioned world models with external policies and reward functions to realize WAM-like decision-making, yet they operate mainly in 2D visual observation space and do not instantiate persistent 3D geometry. Extending this paradigm to 3D games introduces a distinct challenge. In autonomous driving and robotics, the physical environment exists independently of the model, providing a persistent 3D world in which selected actions can be executed. Games have no such external substrate; the virtual world itself must be instantiated. Most playable games require a persistent and navigable space, while 3D games additionally require explicit geometry that supports movement and interaction. Action-conditioned video rollouts provide visual observations but not this spatial representation. We present \textsc{Valerant}, a training-free framework that transforms a pretrained action-conditioned world model into a WAM for exploring and constructing 3D game maps. By coupling predictive visual rollouts with SLAM-based spatial reconstruction and exploration-driven action selection, \textsc{Valerant} progressively transforms a single image into a persistent 3D game map. This framework extends WAM-based interaction beyond 2D visual simulation and offers a new approach to reducing manual effort in 3D game-map creation.

</details>


### [157] [XAI-Arena: Can LLMs Assess the Quality of XAI Explanations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09428)
*Yanfei Hu Fleischhauer,Alona Zharova,Nadja Klein,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 XAI-Arena，一个基于 LLM-as-a-judge 的框架，用于可复现、可扩展、多维度且面向利益相关者的 XAI 解释质量评估，并在多数据集、多模型、多人设条件下进行基准测试，人类验证显示 LLM 评分与人类评分高度正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 XAI 解释质量评估方法多依赖主观人类判断，导致可复现性、可扩展性和研究间可比性受限。作者希望探究 LLM 能否作为可复现且可扩展的机制，对 XAI 解释质量进行比较性评估。

Method: 提出 XAI-Arena，一个 LLM-as-a-judge 评估框架，支持可扩展、可复现、多维度、利益相关者敏感的解释质量评估；从感知简洁性、清晰度、任务充分性、信任校准、可操作性、透明度、忠实性和整体可解释性等维度比较 XAI 解释；并在多种数据集、机器学习模型和利益相关者人设下对 XAI 解释方法进行基准测试；同时进行人类验证。

Result: 人类验证显示 LLM 生成评分与人类评分之间存在强正相关（Spearman's rho=.693, p<.001）。

Conclusion: 基于 LLM 的评估能够捕捉 XAI 解释质量中的系统性差异，并为 XAI 解释的比较评估提供可扩展、可复现的框架。

Abstract: Evaluating the quality of explanations produced by explainable AI (XAI) methods remains challenging because existing approaches often rely on subjective human judgment, limiting reproducibility, scalability, and comparability between studies. We examine whether LLMs can serve as a reproducible and scalable mechanism to make comparative assessments of the quality of XAI explanations. We introduce XAI-Arena, an LLM-as-a-judge framework for scalable, reproducible, multidimensional, and stakeholder-sensitive evaluation of XAI explanation quality. XAI-Arena then allows us to compare XAI explanations along various dimensions, namely, perceived simplicity, clarity, task adequacy, trust calibration, actionability, transparency, faithfulness, and overall interpretability. We then benchmark XAI explanation methods across various datasets, machine learning models, and stakeholder personas. Human validation shows a strong positive association between LLM-generated and human ratings (Spearman's rho=.693, p<.001). Together, LLM-based evaluations can capture systematic differences in XAI explanation quality and provide a scalable and reproducible framework for comparative assessment of XAI explanations.

</details>


### [158] [Do Agents Know When They Succeed? Calibrating Agent Confidence from Internal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09448)
*Priyanka Mary Mammen,Emil Joswin,Srujananjali Medicherla*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多轮智能体任务中，模型内部表示能否作为任务成功与否的预测信号，提出LTD与ARP两种方法，在Bash、SQL、Python三个交互基准和三类模型上均优于表面生成与序列校准基线，实现零开销可靠性监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统在安全关键应用中被快速采用，需要度量其动作置信度。相比传统机器学习，智能体工作流具有规划、工具调用、动态环境交互等复杂失效模式，因此需要更强的成功预测信号。

Method: 提出两种互补方法：Latent Trajectory Dynamics（LTD），通过汇总交互轨迹中残差流表示的变化来刻画过程；Action Representation Probe（ARP），基于动作决策时刻形成的表示预测任务成功。

Result: 在三个交互基准（Bash、SQL、Python）和三类模型家族（Qwen14B、Qwen7B、DeepSeek6.7B）上，两种方法一致优于表面级生成与基于序列的校准基线。

Conclusion: 模型内部表示可提供强于表面信号的任务成功预测，可作为无需修改提示、无需多次采样的零开销可靠性监控器。

Abstract: As agentic systems getting adopted rapidly in safety critical applications, it is vital to measure the confidence associated with the agentic actions. In comparison to the traditional machine learning systems, agentic workflows have complex failure modes with planning, tool invocation and dynamic environment interactions. In this paper, we investigate whether model's internal representations provide stronger signals of eventual task success in multi-turn agentic setups. We introduce two complementary methods: Latent Trajectory Dynamics (LTD), which summarizes changes in residual-stream representations across an an interaction trajectory, and the Action Representation Probe (ARP), which predicts success from representations formed at action decisions. Across three interactive benchmarks (Bash, SQL, Python) and three model families (Qwen14B, Qwen7B, DeepSeek6.7B), our methods consistently outperform surface level generation and sequence-based calibration baselines providing a zero-overhead reliability monitor that requires neither prompt alterations nor multi-sample rollouts.

</details>


### [159] [ContractEval: Query-Conditioned Execution Matching for Procedural Instruction Conformance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09458)
*Praphul Singh,Shanu Kumar,Akshat Agarwal,Ganesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 CONTRACTEVAL，一种将程序性指令建模为"查询激活义务"，并与响应或轨迹证据进行匹配的诊断框架，用于显式化 LLM 智能体执行流程中的隐性合规失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体从回答问题转向执行流程时，失败往往不表现为明显错误：最终响应看似合理，但系统可能跳过了使该答案成立的检查、分支、依赖或不变式。仅看输出的评测只能看到答案，感知轨迹的评判只能看到活动，二者都无法识别哪些义务对该查询是激活的。

Method: 将程序性指令表示为查询激活义务（query-active obligations）；将这些义务与响应或轨迹证据匹配；把遗漏、错误分支、顺序错误、额外动作、不变式违背以及输出契约违规转化为可区分的合规性失败类型。在受控的经审计程序契约套件上评估，对比仅输出与感知轨迹的 LLM 评判器，并在金标准期望图与观测图下测试。

Result: 仅输出与感知轨迹的 LLM 评判器漏检了大量注入的结构性失败；在金标准期望图与观测图下，ContractEval 能够检测并定位全部此类失败。由 LLM 驱动的抽取保留了大部分信号，但对校准较为敏感。

Conclusion: ContractEval 并非合规性保证，而是使程序合规变得可审计，而非隐式地寄托于最终答案质量。

Abstract: As LLM agents move from answering questions to carrying out procedures, failures can be unwarranted rather than visibly wrong: the final response looks acceptable even though the system skipped the check, branch, dependency, or invariant that made the answer justified. Output-only evaluation sees the answer, and trace-aware judging sees activity, but neither identifies which obligations were active for the query. We introduce CONTRACTEVAL, a diagnostic framework for making those active obligations explicit. It represents procedural instructions as query-active obligations and matches them against response or trace evidence, turning omissions, wrong branches, ordering errors, extra actions, invariant breaches, and output-contract violations into distinct conformance failures. On a controlled suite of audited procedural contracts, output-only and trace-aware LLM judges miss many injected structural failures; under gold expected and observed graphs, ContractEval detects and localizes all of them. LLM-backed extraction preserves much of this signal but remains calibration-sensitive. ContractEval is therefore not a compliance guarantee; it makes procedural conformance auditable rather than implicit in final-answer quality.

</details>


### [160] [Multi-Agent Agentic Graph Learning via Structural Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09565)
*Liang Qu,Jianxin Li,Hua Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MAAGL多智能体图学习框架，通过社区划分实现区域专业化，解决图置换不变性与上下文膨胀问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AGL方法（单智能体或多角色智能体）依赖跨图区域共享的推理策略，对结构语义模式异质的图表现次优；而直接引入多智能体协作又面临两大挑战：一是将图结构verbalize为自然语言使推理受结构信息顺序影响，破坏图的置换不变性；二是采样邻域增大会导致上下文迅速膨胀。

Method: MAAGL将图划分为社区并为每个社区分配独立智能体以实现区域特化；结构证据与语义证据分离表示：结构证据由动态更新的、置换不变且定长的结构签名（structural signature）概括，语义证据则按相关性排序过滤至top-k节点；智能体基于相似签名的历史轨迹估计置信度，必要时触发辩论式协作。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，MAAGL优于当前最先进的AGL方法。

Conclusion: MAAGL通过社区级多智能体分工与结构/语义证据的分离表示，兼顾置换不变性与上下文可控性，为异质图推理提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Agentic graph learning (AGL) has recently achieved promising results on graph reasoning tasks, where an agent powered by a large language model (LLM) sequentially samples the graph as evidence to support its final prediction. Existing methods either employ a single agent or orchestrate multiple role-based agents to reason and learn over the entire graph, but both essentially rely on a shared reasoning policy across different graph regions, which can be suboptimal for graphs with heterogeneous structural and semantic patterns. Inspired by the progress of multi-agent collaboration on complex reasoning tasks, a natural remedy is to let multiple agents own different memory and collaborate; however, applying this paradigm to graphs directly faces two challenges. First, existing AGL methods typically verbalize graph structures into natural-language descriptions for LLM agents, making the reasoning process sensitive to the ordering of structural information and thereby breaking the permutation-invariant nature of graphs. Second, incorporating increasingly large sampled neighborhoods leads to rapidly growing contexts. To address these challenges, this paper introduces a multi-agent agentic graph learning (i.e., MAAGL) framework. MAAGL partitions the graph into communities and assigns an independent agent to each community for region-specific specialization. MAAGL represents structural and semantic evidence separately. Structural evidence is summarized by a dynamically updated structural signature that is permutation-invariant and fixed in size, while semantic evidence is filtered to the top-k nodes ranked by relevance. Based on historical trajectories with similar signatures, agents estimate their confidence and trigger debate-style collaboration when needed. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets show that MAAGL outperforms SOTA AGL methods.

</details>


### [161] [CityPlanner: A Sandbox Agent for Executable Urban Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09578)
*Wentao Zhang,Jingyuan Wang,Zetong Zhou,Yifan Yang,Wenrui Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CityPlanner——基于沙箱智能体的可执行城市规划框架，通过UrbanSandbox统一文件环境与原子任务强化学习（BuildPlan/ImprovePlan）实现可学习、可反馈迭代的规划，在真实基准上优于启发式、任务专用RL与通用LLM智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市规划是真实世界的空间优化问题，需在成本、服务质量等现实目标下，从庞大候选空间中选取可行动作。现有优化与强化学习方法虽对固定问题表述有效，但往往依赖任务特定的表示与约束处理方式，泛化性与可迁移性受限。

Method: 提出CityPlanner沙箱智能体框架：(1) UrbanSandbox——统一的基于文件的环境，智能体可检查任务文件、生成方案、运行评估器，并依据可执行反馈修正决策；(2) 原子任务强化学习——将长沙箱轨迹分解为BuildPlan（初始构建）与ImprovePlan（基于反馈的细化）两个原子任务，使学习更易处理。

Result: 在真实世界基准上，CityPlanner持续优于启发式方法、任务专用强化学习方法以及通用LLM智能体基线；消融实验验证了UrbanSandbox、原子任务强化学习与迭代部署各自的贡献。

Conclusion: CityPlanner以统一的沙箱环境与原子任务分解，将城市规划转化为可执行、可反馈、可学习的过程，减少对任务特定表示与约束处理的依赖；代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Urban planning is a real-world spatial optimization problem that requires selecting feasible actions from large candidate spaces under practical objectives such as cost and service quality. Existing optimization and reinforcement learning methods are effective for fixed formulations, but often depend on task-specific representations and constraint handling. We propose \emph{CityPlanner}, a sandbox-agent framework for executable urban planning. CityPlanner introduces \emph{UrbanSandbox}, a unified file-based environment where agents inspect task files, generate plans, run evaluators, and revise decisions based on executable feedback. To make learning tractable, we further propose atomic-task reinforcement learning, which decomposes long sandbox trajectories into \emph{BuildPlan} for initial construction and \emph{ImprovePlan} for feedback-based refinement. Experiments on a real-world benchmark show that CityPlanner consistently outperforms heuristic, task-specific RL, and general LLM-agent baselines. Ablations verify the contributions of UrbanSandbox, atomic-task RL, and iterative deployment. We release the code and dataset at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/co-agent-C1C8

</details>


### [162] [A Function-Space Approach to the Statistical Mechanics of Learning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09589)
*Yizhou Zhang,Weichen Wu,Lun Du,Zhengjie Miao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文在函数空间中建立学习的统计力学描述，将参数配置视为微观实现、函数及其动力学算符视为宏观变量，导出由学习算符M=JJ*与统计算符B控制的涨落自由能Φ，并揭示稳定集体组织的宏观规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络参数空间高度非线性，却表现出规则的宏观行为。作者希望找到一个合适的宏观描述层级，直接在函数空间中刻画学习的稳定集体组织，从而跨越微观参数与宏观动力学之间的鸿沟。

Method: 将参数配置视为微观实现，函数及其动力学算符视为宏观变量。对均方损失，精确误差动力学由学习算符M=JJ*支配。结合条件随机动力学的动力学玻尔兹曼权重与由局部曲率定义的参数空间态密度（统计算符B），对局部涨落积分，得到涨落自由能Φ_fluc(M;B)。

Result: Φ_fluc在固定谱下当[M,B]=0时具有旋转稳定性，且通过将M的大特征值与B的小特征值配对而最小化，并对旋转失配产生局部回复贡献。对ReLU型函数空间在温和稳定统计条件下，B=σ_ξ²L*KL，其中L度量粗粒化二阶结构，因此低B扇区对应低结构曲率，偏好沿平滑、数据自适应方向的更快松弛。

Conclusion: 这些结果确认函数空间是研究学习中稳定集体组织的自然宏观层级，为理解深度网络宏观规律提供了统计力学框架。

Abstract: Deep neural networks exhibit regular macroscopic behavior despite highly nonlinear dynamics in vast parameter spaces. We develop a statistical-mechanical description of learning directly in function space, treating parameter configurations as microscopic realizations and functions with their dynamical operators as macroscopic variables. For mean-squared loss, the exact error dynamics are governed by the learning operator \(M=JJ^\ast\). Combining the dynamical Boltzmann weight of the conditional stochastic dynamics with the parameter-space density of states, whose local curvature defines a statistical operator \(B\), and integrating over local fluctuations yields
  $$ Φ_{\mathrm{fluc}}(M;B)=\frac{σ_ξ^2}{2}\log\det(M^{-1}+B)+\mathrm{const}. $$
  At fixed spectrum, this term is rotationally stationary when \([M,B]=0\), is minimized by pairing large eigenvalues of \(M\) with small eigenvalues of \(B\), and generates a local restoring contribution against rotational mismatch. For ReLU-type function spaces under mild stable statistical conditions, \(B=σ_ξ^2L^\ast\mathcal K L\), where \(L\) measures coarse-grained second-order structure. Thus the low-\(B\) sector corresponds, up to bounded anisotropy of \(\mathcal K\), to low structural curvature, implying a preference for faster relaxation along smooth, data-adaptive directions. These results identify function space as a natural macroscopic level for studying stable collective organization in learning.

</details>


### [163] [From State Synchronization to Cognitive Self-Evolution: An Operational Architecture for Cognitive Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09625)
*Haoran Gao,An Li,Zhen Li,Jun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由物理层、数字孪生层、认知层与任务层构成的四层认知数字孪生（CDT）架构，建立自演化闭环运行回路，并刻画用户请求驱动与自驱动两类认知运行模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CDT研究多聚焦学习模块、知识图谱、大语言模型等单一使能技术，缺乏对认知能力如何系统性融入数字孪生架构的整体认识，缺少结构化架构层面的指导。

Method: 提出四层CDT架构（物理层、数字孪生层、认知层、任务层），构建跨层自演化闭环运行回路：物理状态同步为数字表示，认知层借助知识、记忆与注意力构建任务专用认知模型，在现实约束下生成任务级决策；运行反馈反哺认知经验并更新数字表示中的关系与标注。基于该框架刻画用户请求驱动认知与自驱动认知两种运行模式，并讨论语义通信、知识查询、任务编排、闭环同步等关键使能机制与部署挑战；最后开展轻量级仿真验证。

Result: 轻量级仿真表明，在有限语义信息条件下闭环任务仍具备可靠可行性，并可通过积累任务经验提升运行效率。

Conclusion: 该框架为未来CDT系统的设计与开发提供了结构化的基础，明确了认知能力的层次化集成方式与闭环演化路径。

Abstract: As Digital Twin (DT) systems evolve beyond state synchronization toward task-oriented and knowledge-driven operation, Cognitive Digital Twins (CDTs) have emerged as an extension that incorporates cognitive capabilities into twin operation. Existing CDT studies often focus on specific enabling techniques, such as learning modules, knowledge graphs, and large language models, while providing limited insight into how cognition can be systematically integrated into DT architectures. To address this issue, this paper proposes a four-layer CDT architecture consisting of the physical layer, digital-twin layer, cognitive layer, and task layer. The proposed architecture establishes a self-evolving closed operational loop spanning these four layers, in which physical states are synchronized into digital representations, cognition constructs task-specific cognitive models through knowledge, memory, and attention, and task-level decisions are generated under practical constraints. Operational feedback further refines cognitive experience and updates relationships and annotations in the digital representation, enabling subsequent task interpretation, initiation, and reasoning to evolve with system operation. Based on this framework, two representative operation modes are characterized: user-request-driven cognition and self-driven cognition. We further discuss key enabling mechanisms and deployment challenges associated with semantic communication, knowledge querying, task orchestration, and closed-loop synchronization. A lightweight simulation study illustrates reliable closed-loop task feasibility under limited semantic information and improved operational efficiency through accumulated task experience. The proposed framework provides a structured foundation for the design and development of future CDT systems.

</details>


### [164] [Seven Sources of Physical AI Capability Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09627)
*Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出"能力形成来源"概念，识别出七类非互斥来源（RE、PM、EI、SE、MG、EC、ED），并通过重构性归纳与理论饱和验证其解释力，在既定范围内所有49条证据均可由这七类来源单独或组合解释，未出现不可约的第八类来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有按形态、架构、学习算法、任务或领域划分的分类体系无法直接回答"能力由何而来"这一根本问题，而物理AI相关能力可能源自本质上不同的形成历史，因此需要一个以能力生成为核心的组织框架。

Method: 将"能力形成来源"定义为对能力形成有实质性贡献的因素（区别于组件或构建步骤），识别出七类非互斥来源：记录经验（RE）、预测建模（PM）、评估性交互（EI）、代理环境（SE）、机制基础（MG）、具身耦合（EC）与进化驱动（ED）。采用重构性归纳与理论饱和方法，将研究矩阵追溯至原始研究，去重文献、设定编码规则，并进行三轮最大差异与负例抽样；覆盖课程与自监督学习、主动推理、开放式与发育式学习、规划与搜索、神经符号架构、数字孪生、生成式物理世界模型及形态—控制协同设计等挑战。

Result: 在截至2026年9月4日固定的范围与标准内，全部49条证据记录均可由七类来源单独或组合解释；R1–R3三轮挑战均未产生不可约的第八类来源，且R3无需新增核心定义或实质性边界规则。因此作者声称在既定范围内实现理论饱和，而非逻辑完备性或对未来覆盖的穷尽性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够区分"观察到的能力相似性"与"能力形成方式的相似性"，为解释、迁移、复现、依赖关系、治理证据及地缘经济基础等分析提供支持。

Abstract: Capabilities relevant to Physical AI can arise from materially different formation histories, yet existing taxonomies organized by morphology, architecture, learning algorithm, task, or domain do not directly answer what gives rise to a capability. We define a capability-formation source as a factor materially contributing to capability formation, distinct from components or construction steps. We identify seven non-exclusive sources: Recorded-Experience (RE), Predictive-Modeling (PM), Evaluative-Interaction (EI), Surrogate-Environment (SE), Mechanism-Grounded (MG), Embodied-Coupling (EC), and Evolution-Driven (ED) Formation. Using reconstructive induction with theoretical saturation, we traced a research matrix to primary studies, deduplicated the literature, set coding rules, and conducted three rounds of maximum-difference and negative-case sampling. Challenges included curriculum and self-supervised learning, active inference, open-ended and developmental learning, planning and search, neuro-symbolic architectures, digital twins, generative physical world models, and morphology-control co-design. Within the scope and criteria fixed as of September 4, 2026, all 49 evidence records were explainable by the seven sources individually or in combination. No R1-R3 challenge produced an irreducible eighth source, and R3 required no new core definition or substantive boundary rule. We therefore claim theoretical saturation within the stated scope, not logical completeness or exhaustive future coverage. The framework distinguishes similarity in observed capability from similarity in how it was formed, supporting analysis of explanation, transfer, replication, dependencies, governance evidence, and geoeconomic foundations.

</details>


### [165] [RobustSGPO: Search-Space Control for Agent Harness Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09646)
*Zibo Zhao,Jijun Shi,Mo Zhou,Zhongyuan Wang,Shifu Bie,Yunfei Zhang,Xuanting Zhou,Xiangyu Wu,Bin Liu,Ruiming Tang,Wenwu Ou,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 RobustSGPO，通过明确编辑请求、构建并校验补丁、从现有解或保留快照继续搜索，解决语义梯度提示优化中编辑范围与操作未定的问题，并在 AgentX 流程中验证权限调度、累积控制与任务族迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义梯度提示优化（SGPO）虽能利用执行反馈改进智能体框架，但其局部更新规则未解决“编辑范围”和“编辑操作”的选择问题，导致搜索空间控制不足、迁移与保留行为不明确。

Method: 提出 RobustSGPO：显式指定请求的编辑，构建并检查补丁，并从当前最优解或保留快照继续搜索。在 AgentX 头脑风暴工作流中评估权限调度、累积控制与任务族迁移，使用 120 个任务、95 次运行、7350 次候选尝试。

Result: 周期性 1→2→3 调度比固定最大权限高 0.28 测试分。RobustSGPO 在 30 个留出任务上将完成率从 60.0% 提升至 80.0%，并在 2000 万 token 预算下将测试质量从 3.77 提升至 4.14。类别保留降低迁移后的源任务退化，而随机保留达到更高的目标端点。搜索空间控制通过可执行编辑与替代起点提升质量，并带来可测量的保留开销。

Conclusion: RobustSGPO 通过规范编辑与补丁校验及快照回退机制，有效改进搜索空间控制，在权限调度、累积控制和任务族迁移下均取得质量与完成率提升，同时保留策略存在可测量的开销与权衡。

Abstract: Semantic-gradient-based prompt optimization (SGPO) improves agent harnesses using execution feedback, but its local update rule leaves the choice of edit scope and operation unresolved. We introduce RobustSGPO, which specifies the requested edit, constructs and checks the patch, and continues search from either the incumbent or retained snapshots. We evaluate permission scheduling, cumulative controls, and task-family transfer in the AgentX brainstorming workflow using 120 tasks, 95 runs, and 7,350 candidate attempts. Periodic $1\to2\to3$ scheduling exceeds fixed maximum permission by 0.28 test-score points. RobustSGPO increases completion on 30 held-out tasks from 60.0% to 80.0% and improves test quality from 3.77 to 4.14 under a 20-million-token budget. Category retention reduces source-task degradation after a shift, whereas random retention reaches a higher destination endpoint. Search-space control benefits quality through executable edits and alternative starting points, with measurable retention overhead.

</details>


### [166] [Black-Box Red Teaming of Agentic AI: A Taxonomy-Driven Framework for Automated Risk Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09647)
*Divyanshu Kumar,Nitin Aravind Birur,Tanay Baswa,Sahil Agarwal,Prashanth Harshangi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个黑盒、风险感知的智能体（Agentic）评估框架，仅需基础系统描述即可对多步智能体系统进行安全风险评估。框架包含七域风险分类体系、自动化红队工具 SAGE-RT（每域生成120个对抗场景）以及经人工校验的 LLM 评判评估。在 CrewAI 与 AutoGen 两种架构、四种基座模型上验证，揭示治理风险均值 56.25%、多智能体配置隐私风险 65%、智能体行为漏洞高达 85% 等严重问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统正快速进入生产环境，能够读取不可信输入、以真实权限调用工具并自主行动，使安全攻击面远超纯对话模型。然而现有评估多为单轮对话形式，无法刻画多步骤智能体所面临的漏洞，且通常需要特权访问，难以规模化。因此亟需一种不依赖内部权限、面向多步风险的黑盒评估方法。

Method: 提出系统性黑盒风险感知评估框架，仅需基础系统描述：（1）构建七域风险分类体系（taxonomy），将可观测行为映射到风险类别；（2）设计全自动红队生成方法 SAGE-RT，每个风险域生成120个对抗场景；（3）采用经人工校验的 LLM 评判者（LLM judges）进行评估。在 CrewAI 与 AutoGen 两种智能体架构、四种基座模型上进行实证验证。

Result: 实证结果显示严重的安全隐患：治理（governance）风险平均达 56.25%；多智能体配置下的隐私（privacy）风险达 65%；智能体行为相关漏洞比例高达 85%。

Conclusion: 该黑盒方法无需特权访问即可有效识别智能体系统中的关键架构性漏洞，为更安全的智能体部署提供了一条可扩展的评估路径。

Abstract: Agentic systems are rapidly moving to production, where they read untrusted inputs, call tools with real permissions, and act autonomously, expanding the security surface beyond chat-only models. Yet standard evaluations remain single-turn and fail to capture multi-step agent vulnerabilities. We present a systematic black-box framework for risk-aware agent evaluation requiring only basic system descriptions. Our approach introduces: (1) a seven-domain taxonomy mapping observable behaviors to risk categories, (2) fully automated SAGE-RT red teaming producing 120 adversarial scenarios per domain, and (3) human-validated evaluation using LLM judges. Empirical validation across two agent architectures (CrewAI and AutoGen) with four base models reveals alarming patterns: 56.25\% average governance risk, 65\% privacy risk in multi-agent configurations, and agent behavior vulnerabilities reaching 85\%. Our black-box approach effectively identifies critical architectural vulnerabilities without privileged access, providing a scalable path toward safer agent deployments.

</details>


### [167] [RESCUE-BENCH: Towards Relation-Aware Multi-Party Emotional Support Conversation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09657)
*Haichuan Hu,Yang Xiao,Mingni Tang,Jiawen Duan,Quanjun Zhang,Congqing He,Hao Zhang,Jiashuo Wang,Johan F. Hoorn,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出关系感知情感支持对话（Relation-aware Emotional Support Conversation）新任务，并构建RESCUE基准，评估LLM在多主体场景中捕捉与利用人际关系动态提供情感支持的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感支持对话系统主要聚焦一对一求助者-支持者交互与个体情绪状态，忽视了多方场景中的人际关系，因此需要评估LLM能否利用不断演变的关系动态提供更有效的情感支持。

Method: 从真实的伴侣与家庭访谈对话中构建RESCUE基准，含191个样本、7,079个标注回合、1,064.8分钟视频；基于社会情感与支持动态的丰富标注，设计六个任务，评估关系理解（Relational Understanding）与关系敏感支持（Relation-Sensitive Support）两类核心能力，并对十个LLM进行实验。

Result: 当前模型在依赖局部情绪或干预线索的任务上表现较好，但在关系密集型任务（如关系模式预测、观点预测、支持策略预测）上表现不佳。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在建模人际关系与做出关系敏感的支持决策方面存在明显局限，表明关系感知情感支持对话仍具挑战。

Abstract: Existing emotional support conversation systems mainly focus on one-on-one seeker-supporter interactions and individual emotional states, leaving interpersonal relations in multi-party scenarios underexplored. In this work, we introduce relation-aware emotional support conversation, a new task that evaluates whether LLMs can capture and utilize the evolving dynamics of relationships to offer more effective emotional support. We construct RESCUE (Relation-aware Emotional Support Conversation Understanding and Evaluation Benchmark) from real couple and family interview conversations, containing 191 samples, 7,079 annotated turns, and 1,064.8 minutes of video. Based on rich annotations of socio-emotional and support-related dynamics, RESCUE defines six tasks that evaluate two core capabilities required for relation-aware emotional support: Relational Understanding and Relation-Sensitive Support. Experiments with ten LLMs show that current models perform relatively well on tasks relying on local emotional or intervention cues, but struggle with relation-intensive tasks such as relation pattern prediction, viewpoint prediction, and support strategy prediction. These findings reveal the limitations of current LLMs in modeling interpersonal relations and making relation-sensitive support decisions.

</details>


### [168] [PRAGMA: Evaluating Personalized Guidance with Memory Alignment in Lifelong Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09664)
*Hyojeong Yu,Hyukhun Koh,Minsung Kim,Yunah Jang,Kyomin Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 PRAGMA 基准，用于评估长时对话中的个性化指导能力，发现现有检索系统、记忆系统与长上下文模型在证据恢复与记忆驱动推理上均存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 作为长期个性化助手时，完整交互历史导致计算开销大且难以稳定定位相关信息，因此出现结构化记忆系统。但真实交互中用户更常寻求推荐、规划与决策支持等个性化指导，这类任务需要跨多次对话整合信息并推理用户偏好与经验的变化，而现有对话记忆评测多聚焦检索与事实回忆，缺乏对个性化指导的考察。

Method: 提出 PRAGMA 基准，包含经过整理的纵向对话历史、证据标注，以及在演变中的用户语境与错误用户假设基础上构建的指导场景。在检索系统、记忆系统和长上下文模型上进行实验评估。

Result: 实验表明，当前系统既难以恢复适当的对话证据，也难以有效利用这些证据进行个性化指导。

Conclusion: 结果凸显需要支持稳健对话检索与记忆驱动推理（而非仅证据回忆）的记忆架构，为面向长时对话的个性化指导研究提供了新的评测方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as personalized assistants that interact with users over extended periods of time. As conversations grow longer, relying on full interaction histories becomes increasingly inefficient and unreliable: long contexts introduce substantial computational overhead, making it difficult for models to consistently identify and utilize the most relevant information for the current request. These challenges have motivated memory systems that structure and retrieve user-specific information. In realistic interactions, users often seek practical guidance such as recommendations, planning, and decision support. Unlike factual recall tasks, personalized guidance requires models to integrate information across multiple past conversations and reason about changing user preferences and experiences. However, existing conversational memory evaluations mainly focus on retrieval and factual recall. To study this challenge, we introduce PRAGMA, a benchmark for evaluating personalized guidance in long-term conversations. PRGAMA contains curated longitudinal conversation histories, evidence annotations, and guidance scenarios grounded in evolving user contexts and incorrect user assumptions. Experiments across retrieval systems, memory systems, and long-context models reveal that current systems struggle both to recover the appropriate conversational evidence and to effectively use it for personalized guidance. Our results highlight the need for memory architectures that support robust conversational retrieval and memory-grounded reasoning beyond evidence recall.

</details>


### [169] [Safe to Stop? Risk-Constrained Stopping for Sequential Clinical Diagnosis Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09678)
*Yuexin Wu,Vasile Rus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出风险约束的停止层Cros,将序贯诊断的"何时停止"建模为选择性预测问题,在MIMIC腹痛基准上以有限样本保证换取更低错误率与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床诊断智能体基准多在固定或不加约束的交互后仅评估准确率,忽略自主停止时机,导致"何时诊断/延后"的可靠性缺乏显式度量和统计保证。

Method: Cros由三部分构成:状态级错误排序器(state-wise error ranking)、在不相交开发划分上的策略设计,以及针对完整序贯策略的选择性诊断错误与最小自主覆盖率进行的LTT式精确检验。其有限样本保证要求候选族、检验规则及任何随机化在接触校准标签前冻结。

Result: 在1,834例MIMIC腹痛基准上,完整排序器状态错误AUROC为0.853,优于最大类概率0.715和骨干原生停止分数0.552。在367例已查看评估划分上,对冻结权重解析平均得选择性错误16.9%、覆盖率78.8%、成本5.57、检验次数0.68;原生停止为100%覆盖下错误30.8%、成本8.14、检验1.53次。强制继续呈非单调:仅HPI时错误28.3%,完整检查后34.3%。但均匀权重混合消融在该已查看划分上更便宜,却未满足锁定的开发边际;Cros在20次开发重划分中仅6次名义满足联合准则。

Conclusion: 由于评估标签在早期开发中已被查看,结果仅构成探索性可行性与审计证据,而非确证性安全认证;自主停止的可靠性需要严格冻结流程与独立确认标签。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis agents must decide not only what test to request next, but also when to diagnose or defer. Existing agent benchmarks largely evaluate accuracy after fixed or unconstrained interaction, leaving autonomous stopping reliability implicit. We present Cros, a risk-constrained stopping layer combining state-wise error ranking, policy design on disjoint development splits, and LTT-style exact tests of selective diagnostic error and minimum autonomous coverage for complete sequential policies. Its finite-sample guarantee requires the candidate family, testing rule, and any randomization to be frozen before calibration labels are accessed. On a 1,834-episode MIMIC-derived abdominal-pain benchmark, the full ranker achieves exploratory state-error AUROC 0.853, compared with 0.715 for maximum class probability and 0.552 for the backbone's native stop score. On the previously viewed 367-episode evaluation split, analytically averaging over the frozen Cros weights yields 16.9% selective error at 78.8% coverage, cost 5.57, and 0.68 tests, versus 30.8% error at 100% coverage, cost 8.14, and 1.53 tests under native stopping. Forced continuation is non-monotone: error is 28.3% with HPI alone and 34.3% after full workup. However, the uniform-weight mixture ablation is cheaper on this viewed split despite missing the locked development margins, and Cros nominally satisfies the joint criterion in only 6 of 20 development resplits. Because evaluation labels were inspected during earlier development, these findings provide exploratory feasibility and audit evidence, not a confirmatory safety certificate.

</details>


### [170] [Decision Shifts, Lost Label Functionality, and an Inconclusive Grounding Audit in Correctness-Gated Multi-Teacher Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09702)
*Xiaofei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在固定实验矩阵下检验"正确性门控的多教师蒸馏"，发现加权臂虽在准确率、宏F1与条件不安全率上有提升，但同时丧失标签功能（Refuted召回为零、大量NotEnoughInfo误判），且未优于硬过滤基线；可用的人工审计无法确立grounding增益，故整体结论为"决策重分配但证据支撑性未获证实"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图区分两个常被混同的目标：候选决策正确性（decision correctness）与理由的可溯源性/证据支撑（rationale grounding）。核心动机是探究在多教师蒸馏中加入"正确性门控/加权"能否在提升决策指标的同时改善证据支撑，而不只是重分配决策。

Method: 采用固定实验（fixed-matrix）设计：8个实验臂共享4,330条源数据、一个63.9M参数学生模型、12,990行优化数据、406次更新、证据输入与同一解码器；其中7个基于教师的臂共用同一固定的三响应池。以3个随机种子在267条留出样本上评估。对比"未过滤蒸馏"与"正确性加权臂"（以及硬过滤、源标签SFT等基线），并配套一个人工审计（在单一参考种子上、经可用性修正、20条样本）检查证据支撑情况。

Result: 相对未过滤蒸馏，正确性加权臂：准确率+0.1660（95%观测矩阵区间[0.0670,0.2455]），五标签宏F1 +0.1323（[0.0916,0.1731]），条件不安全动作率-0.4979（[-0.5926,-0.3686]）。但功能损失明显：加权臂在所有种子中Refuted召回为0，两个种子将全部167条claim样例判为NotEnoughInfo。人工审计中加权臂与未过滤臂分别有0/20与1/20证据支持的阳性、20/20与19/20阳性含无支撑内容。硬过滤基线已达0.660准确率、0.530宏F1、0.135条件不安全率，高于源标签SFT（宏F1 0.586）之外的加权臂表现。

Conclusion: 加权臂相对硬过滤未展现可证明的增量决策收益，其表面指标提升实为"决策重分配伴随标签功能丧失"。由于样本非配对、源重叠未序列化、审计修正发生在自动摘要之后但标注之前，该审计无法估计共同来源的grounding效应，对系统级改进或损害均无定论。作者据此将该研究定位为一次固定矩阵的失败分析，并指出可用人工审计尚不足以确立grounding增益。

Abstract: Candidate decision correctness and rationale grounding are different objectives. We examine correctness-gated multi-teacher distillation in a fixed experiment. Eight arms share 4,330 sources, a 63.9M-parameter student, 12,990 optimization rows, 406 updates, evidence inputs, and a decoder; seven teacher-based arms use one fixed three-response pool. Three seeds are evaluated on 267 held-out examples. Relative to unfiltered distillation, the correctness-weighted arm differed in accuracy by +0.1660 (95% observed-matrix interval [0.0670, 0.2455]), five-label macro-F1 by +0.1323 ([0.0916, 0.1731]), and task-defined conditional unsafe-action rate by -0.4979 ([-0.5926, -0.3686]). These shifts do not imply uniformly better behavior. Source-label SFT had the highest mean macro-F1 (0.586). The weighted arm had zero Refuted recall in every seed, and two seeds assigned NotEnoughInfo to all 167 claim examples. In an availability-amended audit at one reference seed, weighted and unfiltered outputs had 0/20 versus 1/20 evidence-supported positives and 20/20 versus 19/20 positives containing unsupported material. Samples were non-paired, source overlap was not serialized, and the amendment followed automatic summarization but preceded annotation. The audit therefore cannot estimate a common-source grounding effect and is inconclusive about system-level improvement or harm. Hard filtering already achieved 0.660 accuracy, 0.530 macro-F1, and 0.135 conditional unsafe rate. The implemented weighted arm showed no demonstrated incremental decision benefit over hard filtering. This fixed-matrix failure analysis shows decision redistribution with lost label functionality; the available human audit does not establish a grounding gain.

</details>


### [171] [Which Tokens Should SFT Actually Learn? A Token-Trimming Perspective on Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09707)
*Yaning Jia,Chunhui Zhang,Wenxuan Xu,Xingjian Diao,Xiaoyuan Wang,Soroush Vosoughi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TrimSFT，一种基于logit gap的token级重加权SFT方法，通过裁剪掉已掌握和弱支持的两端token，将监督集中在中间区域，在六个基座模型和五个数学推理基准上一致优于标准SFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SFT对所有目标token施加统一的交叉熵损失，但不同token对数学推理的学习信号并不等同。统一处理会过度锐化已掌握的token，同时放大低置信token的学习压力，导致训练动态次优。

Method: TrimSFT根据gold token与其最强竞争者之间的logit gap对SFT损失进行token级重加权，裁剪掉两个极端：已掌握token（大logit gap）和弱支持token（小或负logit gap），将学习集中于中间的logit-gap区域。具体以margin m为中心、带宽τ的高斯权重实例化，无需参考模型或额外前向传播。

Result: 在Llama、Qwen和DeepMath家族的六个基座模型及五个数学推理基准上评估，TrimSFT一致优于标准SFT，在其中五个模型上取得最佳平均性能，MATH500上相比SFT提升最高达+26.9分。分析表明带宽τ比margin位置更关键，仅单侧裁剪的半裁剪变体权衡更差。

Conclusion: token级logit-gap分布分析显示，TrimSFT比统一SFT或单调重加权方法更均衡地重塑模型置信度。结果表明推理SFT可受益于同时裁剪两个极端，而非统一对待所有token。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) applies a uniform cross-entropy loss to all target tokens, even though different tokens provide unequal learning signals for mathematical reasoning. This uniform treatment can over-sharpen already mastered tokens while amplifying learning pressure on uncertain, low-confidence tokens, leading to suboptimal training dynamics. We propose Trimmed Logit-Gap SFT (TrimSFT), a simple token-level reweighting method that scales the SFT loss according to the logit gap between the gold token and its strongest competitor. TrimSFT trims supervision away from both extremes: tokens already mastered (large logit gap) and tokens weakly supported by the current model (small or negative logit gap), concentrating learning within an intermediate logit-gap region between them. We instantiate this principle with a Gaussian weight centered at margin m with bandwidth τ, requiring no reference model or additional forward pass. We evaluate TrimSFT on six base models from the Llama, Qwen, and DeepMath families across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks. TrimSFT consistently improves over standard SFT, achieving the best average performance on five out of six models, with gains of up to +26.9 points over SFT on MATH500. Further analyses show that the bandwidth τ matters more than the exact margin location, and that half-trim variants that remove supervision pressure from only one side yield inferior trade-offs. A token-level logit-gap distribution analysis suggests that TrimSFT reshapes model confidence in a more balanced way than uniform SFT or monotonic reweighting methods. These results suggest that reasoning SFT can benefit from trimming both extremes rather than treating all tokens uniformly.

</details>


### [172] [Can Artificial Intelligence Support Healthcare and Mental Health Through Early Cyberbullying Detection ? The Impact of Emotion-Aware AI on Proactive Online Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09735)
*Hamed Jelodar,Amir Firouzi,Yen-Wu Lo,Maryam Tanha,Sajjad Dadkhah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CareGuard——一个面向医疗健康与心理保护的网络欺凌早期预警框架，结合零样本语义标注与微调Transformer模型（BERT、DistilBERT、RoBERTa），并引入情感感知过滤与余弦相似度语义筛选，在保证检测精度的同时提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络欺凌与有害在线交互日益影响医疗系统、心理健康与公共福祉，需要面向医疗健康驱动的心理健康保护与主动在线安全的早期预警机制。

Method: CareGuard融合零样本语义标注与微调Transformer模型（BERT、DistilBERT、RoBERTa），实现跨敏感网络欺凌类别的上下文感知分类；同时引入情感感知过滤机制与基于余弦相似度的语义筛选，以降低医疗监测场景下的冗余计算。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，CareGuard在检测准确率与计算效率之间取得有效平衡。

Conclusion: 该框架在医疗系统、心理健康监测与在线安全应用中具备可扩展部署的潜力。

Abstract: Healthcare systems, mental health, and public well-being are increasingly affected by cyberbullying and harmful online interactions. This paper presents CareGuard, an early-warning framework designed to support healthcare-driven mental health protection and proactive online safety through the detection of cyberbullying-related content using advanced natural language processing techniques. CareGuard integrates zero-shot semantic labeling with fine-tuned transformer-based models, including BERT, DistilBERT, and RoBERTa, to enable robust and context-aware classification across sensitive cyberbullying categories. To improve efficiency and reduce unnecessary computation in healthcare-oriented monitoring settings, the framework incorporates an emotion-aware filtering mechanism alongside cosine similarity-based semantic screening, allowing the system to focus on semantically relevant and emotionally salient content. Experimental results on benchmark datasets demonstrate that CareGuard effectively balances detection accuracy and computational efficiency, highlighting its potential for scalable deployment in healthcare systems, mental health monitoring, and online safety applications.

</details>


### [173] [LexAgentHallu: A Hierarchical Benchmark for Profiling Hallucinations in Legal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09754)
*Yujin Zhou,Mingxuan Zheng,Chuxue Cao,Huang Yidan,Jiale Chen,Yike Guo,Sirui Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LexAgentHallu，一个面向法律智能体的细粒度幻觉评测基准，用于诊断多步执行轨迹中"如何"与"多大程度"发生幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律智能体在工具调用与推理中会产生级联式幻觉，导致捏造裁判结论与虚假引证；但现有法律基准仅做单轮问答与结果级评测，通用智能体幻觉基准又缺乏法律专业诊断能力，无法回答幻觉在轨迹中"程度如何、如何发生"。

Method: 采用四阶段专家在环流水线构建 LexAgentHallu，涵盖 3414 个实例、17 个法律类别与 6 类任务；每个实例按双层幻觉分类体系（7 个高层类别、27 个细粒度子类）标注，同时覆盖实质错误与智能体流程性失败；并设计细粒度指标以量化程度并定位轨迹中的失败位置。

Result: 在 18 个闭源与开源智能体上评测，发现"答案正确但推理错误"（Right-Answer-Wrong-Reason）现象；幻觉子类呈聚集而非离散分布，形成可区分的智能体框架、法律任务与类别画像。

Conclusion: 这些在结果级评测中不可见的发现验证了 LexAgentHallu 在法律智能体幻觉诊断上的有效性与诊断力。

Abstract: As large language models are increasingly deployed as tool-augmented legal agents, they introduce agentic hallucinations where tool-call and reasoning errors cascade into fabricated holdings and miscited authority.
