The number of mistakes is then bounded, for all three, by a quantity independent of $T$; and for the online Newton step and for MetaGrad with SGS, the dimension dependence of the regret becomes $O(d^2)$ when the forward problem is an integer linear program, that is, the factor $\log T$ is removed. Moreover, when the action set is M-convex, the regret is bounded efficiently without computing a center of gravity.

</details>


### [241] [In Medical Claims Data, Enhancing Predictive Performance for Major Adverse Cardiovascular Events Using Cross Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09824)
*Yuhei Fujioka,Daitaro Misawa,Tatsuyoshi Ikenoue,Shingo Fukuma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究针对医疗索赔数据中临床信息高度非结构化的问题，提出基于交叉注意力机制的方法对诊断与治疗之间的关系进行加权表征，从而生成更具代表性的特征以预测主要不良心血管事件（MACE），其ROC-AUC达0.7720，优于传统ASCVD模型、LightGBM及自注意力模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗索赔数据同时包含费用、账单等财务信息以及诊断、治疗等临床信息，近年来已被用于构建大规模医学研究数据库。然而其中的临床信息往往是非结构化的，限制了其在综合分析中的应用。同时，MACE是全球主要死因之一，相关预测模型对临床实践指南具有重要意义，因此需要更有效地表征索赔数据中的临床结构以提升预测性能。

Method: 研究采用交叉注意力（cross-attention）机制，构建能够有效加权诊断与治疗之间关系的方法。通过对医疗索赔数据中的临床信息进行结构化表征，生成更具代表性的特征用于MACE预测。并与多种基准模型进行对比，包括传统动脉粥样硬化性心血管疾病（ASCVD）模型、LightGBM以及基于自注意力（self-attention）的模型。

Result: 所提出的基于交叉注意力的模型ROC-AUC为0.7720，高于传统ASCVD模型、LightGBM和自注意力模型等基准模型。

Conclusion: 利用交叉注意力机制整合医疗索赔数据的临床结构，能够显著提升预测模型的性能，表明该方法在MACE预测及医疗索赔数据临床信息利用方面具有有效性和应用价值。

Abstract: Medical claims data comprise the financial details, including the expenses and billing information, as well as the clinical information, such as the diagnoses and treatments, of patients visiting medical facilities. Recently, it has been acknowledged that large databases can be constructed from medical claims data for medical research purposes. However, the clinical information within these datasets is often medically unstructured, limiting its application in comprehensive analyses. This study enhances predictive model performance for major adverse cardiovascular events (MACE), a leading cause of death worldwide. Models that predict MACE are crucial to clinical practice guidelines. We utilize a cross-attention mechanism to develop a method that effectively weights the relationships between diagnoses and treatments. Effectively repre- senting the clinical information contained in medical claims data, this approach generates more representative features for predicting MACE. The ROC-AUC score of our proposed cross-attention-based model was 0.7720, higher than other benchmark models including the conventional atherosclerotic cardiovascular disease model, the light gradient boosting machine, and a self-attention-based model. These results indicate that integrating the clinical structure of medical claims data using a cross-attention mechanism significantly enhances the performance of predictive models.

</details>


### [242] [TempTPI: Informer-Based trajectory prediction for maritime vessels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09840)
*Kevin Ferneding,Veronika Lietavcova,Aleksandra M. Blachowiak,Peder Heiselberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TempTPI框架，结合Informer编码器与多通道时间编码，实现长时程船舶轨迹预测，在丹麦海域AIS数据上5小时预测窗口MSE较TPTrans提升55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上船舶长时程轨迹预测对航行安全与物流效率至关重要。现有基于深度学习的AIS轨迹预测方法（尤其是Transformer）存在两大问题：自注意力机制的二次计算复杂度导致计算开销大，且随预测时程延长精度显著下降。

Method: TempTPI框架由两部分构成：(1) 基于Informer的编码器，采用ProbSparse稀疏自注意力机制降低计算复杂度并聚焦最关键依赖；(2) 多通道时间编码机制，利用类傅里叶频率展开捕获船舶行为中的周期性模式（小时、日、季节尺度）。

Result: 在丹麦海域AIS数据上与SOTA方法TPTrans对比，TempTPI在1至5小时各预测窗口上均一致优于现有方法；尤其在5小时预测时程下，MSE指标提升55%。

Conclusion: TempTPI通过稀疏注意力与周期性时间编码的结合，有效缓解了长时程预测中的算力与精度瓶颈，为长距离海上态势感知提供了鲁棒解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate long-term trajectory prediction for maritime vessels is essential for safety and logistical efficiency. While deep learning models, particularly Transformers, have shown promise in processing Automatic Identification System (AIS) data, they often struggle with the quadratic computational complexity of self-attention and the loss of accuracy over extended forecasting horizons. This study proposes TempTPI, a novel prediction framework that integrates an Informer-based encoder with a multi-channel temporal encoding mechanism. The Informer architecture leverages a ProbSparse self-attention mechanism to reduce computational overhead and focus on the most significant dependencies, while the temporal encoder utilizes Fourier-like frequency expansions to capture cyclic patterns (hourly, daily, and seasonal) in vessel behavior. We evaluate our model against the state-of-the-art TPTrans architecture using AIS data from Danish waters. Experimental results demonstrate that TempTPI consistently outperforms existing methods across prediction windows of 1 to 5 hours. Notably, at a 5-hour horizon, the proposed model achieves a 55% improvement in Mean Squared Error (MSE), offering a robust solution for long-range maritime situational awareness.

</details>


### [243] [Exact Degeneracy Under Balanced k-Shot Sampling:Consequences for Small-Sample Discriminant Analysis on LLM Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09860)
*Lingxiao Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 平衡k-shot采样使KLPCDA的类内散布算子退化为精确的缩放正交投影，导致其部分变体特征值完全相等或目标函数失效；修复后仍不及交叉验证的逻辑回归探针，且高维解码器嵌入的劣势主要源于估计效率而非几何固有缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡k-shot采样被广泛用于少样本学习，但其对判别式估计器（尤其是KLPCDA）造成的精确退化机制尚未被理论刻画；同时像素、振动信号、基因表达数据上的降维指导能否迁移到LLM嵌入空间存疑。

Method: 理论推导平衡采样下KLPCDA类内散布算子的投影结构并给出闭式后果，提出公式内平局打破（tie-break）修复；在冻结句子嵌入与解码器残差流激活上验证，随后在四个数据集、三种嵌入维度、k≤10至30–50下，与SetFit、LoRA、ICL及交叉验证逻辑回归对比，并用三种几何可分性指标做诊断。

Result: 两个KLPCDA变体的信号特征值精确相等导致特征向量选择准则可证明地无差异，第三个变体目标函数空洞；tie-break修复后，残余子空间约束在少类数据集上的代价是多类数据集的5倍（p=0.000001）；交叉验证逻辑回归在四个数据集中三个、所有嵌入维度上均优于全部KLPCDA变体；三种几何指标无法解释高维解码器嵌入弱于小型双向编码器，k从≤10增至30–50时差距缩小逾80%（p=0.00195）。

Conclusion: KLPCDA的退化源于估计器构造而非数据集特性；传统降维经验不能直接迁移到LLM嵌入空间，高维解码器嵌入的性能差距是估计效率问题而非永久性上限。

Abstract: Balanced k-shot sampling draws exactly k labeled examples per class. We show that it induces an exact, provable degeneracy in a family of small-sample discriminant estimators. Under balanced sampling, the within-class scatter operator of Kernelized Linear Principal Component Discriminant Analysis (KLPCDA) is not merely rank-deficient but exactly a scaled orthogonal projector. We derive the consequences in closed form: two of KLPCDA's seven variants have every signal eigenvalue exactly equal, so their eigenvector selection criterion is provably indifferent rather than ill-conditioned, and a third has a provably void objective. This follows from the estimators' construction, not any dataset; we confirm it on frozen sentence embeddings and, separately, on residual-stream activations from a decoder-only generative model. An in-formula tie-break repairs the two repairable variants, with recovery gated by class count: the residual subspace constraint costs 5x more on few-class than many-class datasets (p=0.000001).
  We then evaluate the repaired framework on few-shot text classification on frozen LLM embeddings (n much smaller than d, up to 4096), across four datasets, three embedding sizes, and three trained baselines (SetFit, LoRA, in-context learning). A properly cross-validated logistic-regression probe still beats every KLPCDA variant on three of four datasets, at every embedding size; guidance carried from pixel, vibration-signal, and gene-expression data does not directly generalize to this feature space. Three independent geometric separability metrics fail to explain why one high-dimensional decoder-based embedding model underperforms smaller bidirectional encoders, ruling out anisotropy; the gap is substantially an estimation-efficiency effect, not a permanent ceiling, closing by more than 80% when the support set grows from k<=10 to k=30-50 (p=0.00195, both many-class datasets).

</details>


### [244] [Forward-Free LLM Depth Pruning via Weight Redundancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09883)
*Vincent-Daniel Yun,Woosang Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出免前向传播的深度剪枝方法 WRP，利用权重估计层间冗余来选取并移除 Transformer 块，无需校准数据与模型前向，性能优于现有免前向方法并接近基于激活的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度剪枝通过移除完整 Transformer 块降低 LLM 推理成本。基于激活的方法需在校准数据上做前向传播收集隐状态，而现有免前向方法只是单独对每个块打分，未度量块间相似性，因而无法捕捉层间冗余。

Method: WRP（Weight-Redundancy Pruning）是免前向的深度剪枝方法：从 checkpoint 权重估计层间冗余，比较各层的注意力输出权重与 MLP 下投影权重，将二者的成对相似度与相对投影尺度信息结合，构建全对相似度矩阵，据此进行层分组与块选择，全程无需校准数据和模型前向。

Result: 在多种剪枝设置、模型家族和下游任务上，WRP 一致优于现有免前向的幅度剪枝方法，并接近基于激活方法的性能。

Conclusion: 权重中的层间相似度可作为冗余代理，使免前向深度剪枝在无需校准数据的情况下达到接近基于激活方法的剪枝效果。

Abstract: Depth pruning reduces large language model (LLM) inference cost by removing complete Transformer blocks. Activation-based methods collect hidden states through forward passes on calibration data, while existing forward-free methods score each Transformer block separately without measuring similarity between blocks. We propose Weight-Redundancy Pruning (WRP), a forward-free depth-pruning method that estimates inter-layer redundancy from checkpoint weights to select blocks without calibration data or model forward passes. WRP compares attention output and MLP down-projection weights across layers and combines their pairwise similarities with relative projection-scale information. The resulting all-pairs similarity matrix guides layer grouping and block selection. Across multiple pruning settings, model families, and downstream tasks, WRP consistently outperforms existing forward-free magnitude pruning and approaches the performance of activation-based methods.

</details>


### [245] [ProMeta: Few-shot PROTAC-targeted degradation prediction across E3 ligases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09891)
*Yuansheng Liu,Yufei Ye,Tao Tang,Jiawei Luo,Wen Tao,Xiao Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将跨E3连接酶的PROTAC降解活性预测重构为小样本元学习问题，提出基于原型的图神经网络ProMeta，在CRBN↔VHL跨连接酶基准上显著优于监督GNN基线，实现双向可迁移的小样本预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PROTAC作为靶向降解"不可成药"靶点的策略受到关注，但现有监督式降解活性预测方法受限于数据稀缺与E3连接酶间分布不均。标注数据集中于CRBN、VHL等少数连接酶，多数连接酶研究不足却对拓展降解剂设计空间至关重要，因此亟需在极少标注下实现稳健跨连接酶泛化的方法。

Method: 将跨E3连接酶的PROTAC降解活性预测重构为小样本元学习问题。提出ProMeta：基于原型的图神经网络，在源E3任务上以情景式（episodic）元学习训练，在留出的目标E3任务上通过支持集条件化推理评估。推理时无需更新编码器，仅由目标连接酶的少量支持样本动态估计类原型。

Result: CRBN→VHL基准上，K=2,Q=3时AUROC达0.796，K=2,Q=5时达0.883，较监督GNN基线分别提升19.9%和6.8%。反向VHL→CRBN迁移在相同协议下AUROC为0.702（K=2,Q=3）和0.821（K=2,Q=5），验证了双向适用性，同时揭示出方向与数据规模依赖性。

Conclusion: 在所评估的支持/查询协议下，ProMeta可作为跨连接酶小样本预测的实用框架。结果支持其在极少标注条件下跨E3连接酶泛化的有效性，但性能存在迁移方向和样本量依赖，仍属特定协议下的验证结果。

Abstract: Proteolysis-targeting chimeras (PROTACs) have emerged as a transformative therapeutic strategy that selectively degrades historically ''undruggable'' targets via the ubiquitin-proteasome system. Despite growing efforts to develop computational predictors of PROTAC degradation activity, existing supervised approaches remain severely challenged by data scarcity and imbalance across E3 ligases, limiting their ability to generalize beyond well-studied ligase contexts. In practice, labeled data are heavily concentrated on a few ligases (e.g., CRBN and VHL), while the majority of E3 ligases remain underexplored yet are critical for expanding the design space of targeted degraders. Developing methods that enable robust cross-ligase generalization with minimal labeled data is therefore essential for improving the practical utility of computational PROTAC discovery. We reformulate PROTAC degradation activity prediction across E3 ligases as a few-shot meta-learning problem and present ProMeta, a prototype-based graph neural network trained through episodic meta-learning on source-E3 tasks and evaluated on held-out target-E3 tasks through support-conditioned inference. ProMeta performs inference without updating the encoder by dynamically estimating class prototypes from minimal target-ligase support samples. On the CRBN-to-VHL benchmark, ProMeta achieves AUROC values of 0.796 under K=2, Q=3 and 0.883 under K=2, Q=5, improving by 19.9% and 6.8%, respectively, over the corresponding supervised GNN baseline. Reverse VHL-to-CRBN transfer under the same protocol yielded AUROC values of 0.702 (K=2, Q=3) and 0.821 (K=2, Q=5), confirming bidirectional applicability while revealing direction and data-regime dependence. Together, these results support ProMeta as a practical framework for cross-ligase few-shot prediction under the evaluated support/query protocols.

</details>


### [246] [Strangers to Themselves: What Language Models Say About Themselves Is Generic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09899)
*Phil Blandfort,Urja Pawar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究将"模型自我认知"转化为预测检验：让模型预测自身在九类行为评估中的表现，并与去掉"自我"的对照条件比较。结果显示自我报告预测力极弱，且与对"通用AI代理"的预测效果相当，其他模型的自我描述甚至同样能预测目标模型；第一人称框架只会让报告更"悦己"（低报有害行为）；微调可教出狭窄的自我预测但会改变被预测行为且不具泛化性。结论是模型被问"你会怎么做"时，给出的主要是关于AI助手的一般性理论加上有利偏差，而非关于自身的特权知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型能流畅描述自己会如何行为（是否屈从压力、滥用工具或在压力下撒谎），但这种描述是否真的反映该模型自身，尚不清楚。作者希望检验"自我知识"是否真实存在：若自我报告确有特权性，模型对自己行为的预测应显著优于对他人/泛化对象的预测。此前缺乏将自我知识转化为可量化预测能力的系统检验。

Method: 把自我知识操作化为预测任务。在九项行为评估中，先测量模型在不同条件下的真实行为率，再让模型预测这些比率（直接自我报告、展示具体题项后的自我报告），并设置去掉"自我"的对照组：关于"通用能干AI代理"的同题项提问、其他模型的自我报告。同时考察前沿规模是否改变该模式、第一人称框架的影响，以及用模型自身行为记录进行微调能否教出自我预测。

Result: (i) 直接自我报告预测力很弱（r = +0.04），即使展示确切题项也仅提升至 +0.24；关键是对"通用能干AI代理"的同题项提问效果相当（+0.28），而其他模型关于自身的回答对目标模型的预测力至少不低于其自身。(ii) 前沿规模未明显改变该模式，预测力的提升并非自我特异，更符合"关于AI助手如何行为的更好理论"而非更好的自我知识。(iii) 第一人称框架有一个稳健效应：使报告向悦己方向偏移，相对对通用代理的同一问题低报有害行为。(iv) 用模型自身行为记录微调可教出狭窄的自我预测，但同时改变了被预测的行为，且收益不能广泛迁移。

Conclusion: 模型被问及"自己会怎么做"时，主要揭示的是关于AI助手的一般性理论加上一种有利偏差，而非对该模型的特权性知识。因此把自我报告当作自省式证据需谨慎；自我知识更多是泛化的行为理论而非自我特异信息。

Abstract: Language models can fluently describe how they would behave: whether they would cave to pushback, misuse a tool, or lie under pressure. Is that description actually about the model speaking? We turn self-knowledge into a prediction test. Across nine behavioral evaluations, we measure how a model behaves under different conditions, ask it to predict those rates, and compare its predictions with controls that remove the self from the question. We find that: (i) Direct self-report is weak (r = +0.04), and even showing the model the exact items only raises prediction to +0.24. Crucially, the same item-informed question about "capable AI agents in general" does just as well (+0.28), while other models' answers about themselves predict the target model at least as well as its own. (ii) Frontier scale does not detectably change this pattern: any gains in prediction are not self-specific, and are consistent with a better theory of how AI assistants behave rather than better self-knowledge. (iii) First-person framing does have one robust effect: it shifts reports in the flattering direction, understating harmful behavior relative to the same question about a generic agent. (iv) Finetuning on a model's own behavioral record can teach narrow self-predictions, but it also changes the behavior being predicted and the gains do not transfer broadly. The practical implication is simple: asking a model what it would do mostly reveals a theory of AI assistants in general, plus a favorable bias, rather than privileged knowledge of that model.

</details>


### [247] [Beyond Conventional Federated Learning via High-Order Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09904)
*Alireza Kabgani,Masoud Ahookhosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HiFedProx，用尺度匹配的幂型正则化（p≥2）替代FedProx的二次惩罚，在FEMNIST压力实验中p=5–7性能最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦客户端多步本地优化会产生量级差异很大的参数位移，FedProx的二次正则随位移线性增长，难以有效控制普通位移与异常大位移之间的对比。

Method: 以幂型正则化（指数p≥2）替换二次惩罚，所有幂次在参考位移R处具有相同的正则化梯度幅值；p>2时在R以下响应更弱、R以上更强。结合有限预算的随机客户端优化与同小批量Armijo回溯。在冻结的60-writer FEMNIST子集上做五种子配对实验，并在p∈{2,…,8}上做公共参数研究。

Result: 干净训练性能相近，但复合压力下显著提升。中等与严重压力下最低损失分别在p=7和p=6，较p=2分别改善11.44%与23.16%。位移尾部比值随p=8持续下降，但预测性能在中间范围达到峰值，且Armijo试验成本随p增大而增加。

Conclusion: 幂指数应校准而非最大化，p=5–7为最有用的取值范围。

Abstract: Federated clients that perform several local optimization steps can return parameter displacements with widely different magnitudes. The quadratic regularization of FedProx grows linearly with displacement and therefore offers limited control over the contrast between ordinary and unusually large client movements. We here introduce HiFedProx, which replaces the quadratic penalty with a scale-matched power-type regularizer indexed by $p\geq2$. All powers have the same regularization-gradient magnitude at a reference displacement $R$, while every $p>2$ gives a weaker response below $R$ and a stronger response above it. An exact affine reference calculation shows that increasing $p$ compresses relative displacement disparities, although very large powers approach fixed-radius behavior and increase local curvature. HiFedProx combines this geometry with finite-budget stochastic client optimization and same-minibatch Armijo backtracking. In paired five-seed experiments on a frozen 60-writer FEMNIST subset, a common-parameter study over $p\in\{2,3,4,5,6,7,8\}$ shows similar clean-training performance but substantial gains under composite stress. The lowest moderate- and severe-stress losses occur at $p=7$ and $p=6$, improving over $p=2$ by $11.44\%$ and $23.16\%$, respectively. Although displacement-tail ratios continue to decrease through $p=8$, predictive performance peaks in an intermediate range and Armijo trial cost increases with $p$. These results indicate that the exponent should be calibrated rather than maximized. In our experiments, $p=5$--$7$ provides the most useful range.

</details>


### [248] [Meta-LinEXP3: Online-within-Online Learning for Adversarial Linear Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09907)
*Hao Li,Jie Xu,Zheng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Meta-LinEXP3，首个针对随机动作集对抗线性上下文赌博机（ALCB）的元学习算法，通过在线嵌套在线（online-within-online）框架构建任务级先验引导内层LinEXP3学习器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机赌博机与非上下文对抗赌博机的元学习已有较多进展，但随机动作集下的对抗线性上下文赌博机（ALCB）元学习尚属空白，亟需填补这一理论与方法缺口。

Method: 提出Meta-LinEXP3在线嵌套在线算法：从已完成任务构造可预测的任务级先验以指导内层LinEXP3。针对已知上下文分布设计策略中心估计器；针对未知分布引入仅依赖历史的正则化矩估计器，并建立先验精度与迁移遗憾间的直接联系。

Result: 已知分布下达到基于内在维度的每任务遗憾界 O(√n)；未知分布下主导遗憾项为 O(n^{2/3})，并给出显式有限样本误差。理论上证明先验越准确则迁移依赖遗憾越次线性。实验验证了算法有效性，并应用于结构化高光谱张量采样。

Conclusion: Meta-LinEXP3为随机动作集ALCB元学习提供了统一框架与理论保证，揭示了先验精度与迁移遗憾的定量关系，并在实际采样任务中展现出良好效果。

Abstract: Meta-learning has emerged as an effective paradigm for transferring knowledge across sequential bandit tasks. While substantial progress has been made for stochastic bandits and non-contextual adversarial bandits, meta-learning for adversarial linear contextual bandits (ALCBs) with random action sets remains largely unexplored. To address this problem, we propose Meta-LinEXP3, an online-within-online algorithm that constructs a predictable task-level prior from completed tasks to guide the inner LinEXP3 learner. For known context distributions, we develop a policy-centered estimator that achieves an intrinsic-dimension $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{n})$ per-task regret bound. For unknown distributions, we introduce a past-only regularized moment estimator with an $\mathcal{O}(n^{2/3})$ leading regret term and explicit finite-sample error. We further establish a direct connection between prior accuracy and transfer regret, showing that increasingly accurate priors yield sublinear transfer-dependent regret across tasks. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of Meta-LinEXP3, including its application to structured hyperspectral tensor sampling.

</details>


### [249] [A Kernel-Based Modular Discriminant Analysis Framework for Small-Sample Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09910)
*Lingxiao Qu,Yan Pei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对KLPCDA进行系统性跨域研究，分析其三个核心目标（方差、类间、类内）的交互机制，揭示七种变体在多种小样本任务中的表现规律，并给出变体选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小样本（SSS）问题因成本、可获取性或伦理限制导致标注数据稀缺，是机器学习的基础挑战。现有方法在高维、少样本条件下难以维持稳定且具判别性的表示。KLPCDA虽初步有效，但其各组件在不同SSS场景中的交互作用缺乏系统理解。

Method: 对KLPCDA进行系统性跨域研究，分析其三个核心目标（方差保留、类间可分性、类内紧凑性）的交互机制；在多种真实SSS任务上考察七种变体行为；通过大量实验与消融研究，分析不同目标组合在噪声、类别不平衡、高维等条件下的性能影响。

Result: 实验揭示三个核心目标交互的一致规律；KLPCDA在各领域均取得稳健且强的性能，同时保持低计算复杂度，适合资源受限环境；并据此导出面向不同数据特征的变体选择实用指南。

Conclusion: KLPCDA的目标交互具有统一且可解释的模式，为SSS场景下稳定表示与判别增强提供理解，并形成可操作的变体选择准则，兼具跨域鲁棒性与低计算成本。

Abstract: The small-sample-size (SSS) problem remains a fundamental challenge in machine learning when labeled data are scarce due to cost, accessibility, or ethical constraints. While numerous approaches have been proposed, existing methods often struggle to maintain stable and discriminative representations under high-dimensional and limited-data conditions. Kernelized Linear Principal Component Discriminant Analysis (KLPCDA), a recently proposed modular framework, integrates variance preservation, inter-class separability, and intra-class compactness within a unified kernel space. Although its formulation has shown promising initial results, a systematic understanding of how its components interact across diverse SSS scenarios remains lacking. In this paper, we present a systematic cross-domain study of KLPCDA to characterize the interaction mechanisms among its core objectives. We analyze the behavior of its seven variants across multiple real-world SSS tasks, including hyperspectral image classification, mechanical fault diagnosis, medical diagnosis, and face recognition. Through extensive experiments and ablation studies, we investigate how different objective combinations influence performance under varying conditions such as noise, class imbalance, and high dimensionality. Our analysis reveals consistent patterns in the interaction of the three core objectives variance, between-class, and within-class terms, providing a unified and interpretable understanding of their roles in stabilizing representations and enhancing discrimination in SSS settings. Based on these findings, we further derive practical guidelines for selecting appropriate KLPCDA variants under different data characteristics. Experimental results demonstrate that KLPCDA achieves strong and robust performance across domains, while maintaining low computational complexity suitable for resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [250] [Development and Validation of a Physics-Guided Machine Learning Extrapolation Framework Using a Classical Transient Diffusion Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09912)
*Ashutosh Yadav,Alok Dubey,Prodyut Ranjan Chakraborty,Harshal Akolekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合物理引导架构的机器学习外推框架，用于训练域之外的可靠预测，并以一维瞬态扩散基准问题验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程中的机器学习模型通常仅在有限工况范围内训练，但实际常需在训练域之外进行可靠预测，因此核心挑战是外推而非内插。训练范围之外数据稀缺，导致严格验证困难。

Method: 将新型外推框架与既有机器学习架构集成，系统评估两种物理引导架构：双向长短期记忆网络（BiLSTM）与物理信息神经网络（PINN）。以经典一维瞬态扩散问题为基准，因其存在精确解析解，可提供全域无限制可靠数据。引入物理引导坐标变换、边界感知学习策略与增强稳定性的时间推进方法。采用“训练—预测—验证—扩展”策略，将验证后的预测递归加入训练集以逐步扩展预测范围。

Result: 在训练域之外实现了准确且物理一致的预测。该问题从初始奇异性经强非线性瞬态演化至稳态线性分布；当训练数据局限于中间段时，向奇异性方向的后向外推尤为困难，框架仍表现良好。

Conclusion: 该框架在数据可获得性有限的工程应用中具有潜力，可实现训练域之外准确且物理一致的预测。

Abstract: Machine learning models used in engineering are typically trained within limited operating ranges, yet reliable predictions are often required beyond these domains. Consequently, the primary challenge is extrapolation rather than interpolation. Rigorous validation is hindered by the scarcity of data outside the training range. To address this limitation, a novel extrapolation framework is integrated with established machine learning architectures to enable accurate and physically consistent predictions beyond the training domain. The framework is established by systematically evaluating two physics-guided architectures: a Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) network and a Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN). A classical one-dimensional transient diffusion problem is adopted as a benchmark because its exact analytical solution provides unlimited, reliable data across the spatio-temporal domain, enabling rigorous quantitative validation. The problem is particularly challenging because the solution evolves from an initial singularity through a strongly nonlinear transient regime before approaching a steady-state linear profile. When training data are confined to an intermediate portion of this evolution, backward extrapolation toward the singularity becomes especially demanding. To improve reliability, physics-guided coordinate transformations, boundary-aware learning strategies, and stability-enhancing temporal marching are incorporated. Extrapolation is evaluated using a train-predict-validate-extend strategy, in which validated predictions are recursively added to the training set to progressively extend the prediction horizon. The results demonstrate accurate and physically consistent predictions beyond the training domain, highlighting the framework's potential for engineering applications where data availability is limited.

</details>


### [251] [Multi-Pass, Multi-View Blended Learning for High-Fidelity Volumetric CT Synthesis from Chest X-Rays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09920)
*Ozer Can Devecioglu,Serkan Kiranyaz,Rashid Mazhar,Tahir Hamid,Muhammad Chowdhury,Moncef Gabbouj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多轮多视角混合学习框架，实现从真实胸片直接合成高保真体CT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张2D胸片重建3D CT是病态逆问题，且配对的CXR-CT数据稀缺。现有方法用CT生成的DRR合成投影作训练，但DRR与真实CXR存在域差异，泛化受限，临床图像重建粗糙且解剖不一致。

Method: Multi-Pass Multi-View Blended Learning框架，分两阶段递进：Stage 1为无监督CXR到DRR域适应；Stage 2含三遍，即(a)有监督DRR到CT变换、(b)无监督多视角切片细化、(c)渐进迁移学习(PTL)。

Result: 在提供配对DRR-CT真值的LIDC-IDRI数据集上，PSNR最高提升14%，SSIM提升7.6%。

Conclusion: 该框架能从真实CXR生成结构一致、解剖真实的高保真CT体积，缓解合成到真实域差异，推动该任务向临床可用性迈进。

Abstract: Reconstructing volumetric Computed Tomography (CT) from a single 2D chest radiograph (CXR) is an ill-posed inverse problem, further complicated by the scarcity of paired CXR-CT training data. Prior approaches address this by training on Digitally Reconstructed Radiographs (DRRs), which are synthetic projections derived from CT volumes. However, the domain gap between DRRs and real CXRs limits generalization, often resulting in coarse or anatomically inconsistent reconstructions when applied to clinical images. To address this challenging problem, this study introduces a Multi-Pass Multi-View Blended Learning framework for synthesizing high-fidelity volumetric CT directly from real chest X-ray (CXR) images. The proposed approach progressively decomposes the synthesis task into two distinct, complementary learning stages. Stage 1 is an unsupervised CXR-to-DRR Domain Adaptation, while Stage 2 includes three passes, namely, (a) supervised DRR-to-CT Transformation, (b) unsupervised Multi-View Slice Refinement, followed by (c) Progressive Transfer Learning (PTL). With such a blended learning paradigm, the proposed approach mitigates the synthetic-to-real domain gap while enhancing both the structural integrity and anatomical detail of the final output. On the LIDC-IDRI dataset, where paired DRR-CT ground truth is available for quantitative evaluation, the proposed method improves upon prior methods by up to 14% in PSNR and 7.6% in SSIM. The framework successfully generates structurally consistent and anatomically realistic high-fidelity CT volumes from real CXRs, marking a significant advancement toward clinical viability of CT reconstruction from standard radiographic images.

</details>


### [252] [Adversarial Training for Tabular Credit Scoring: A Multi-Attack Robustness Evaluation in P2P Lending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09945)
*Gijs A. F. Niewzwaag,Marijn G. S. Veth,Manuele Massei,Marcos R. Machado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对P2P信贷机器学习评分模型在对抗性操纵下的鲁棒性问题，基于Lending Club数据构建系统性训练-测试鲁棒性基准，发现单一攻击防御会高估真实鲁棒性，混合训练在异质攻击下表现最均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习信用评分在P2P借贷中日益核心，但申请人策略性修改自报特征以获取有利决策的对抗鲁棒性尚不清楚；现有对抗鲁棒性证据多来自图像与文本领域，且通常只评估单一攻击对应匹配防御，无法说明表格信用数据中防御跨攻击类型的泛化能力。

Method: 在Lending Club大规模子集上开展系统性训练-测试鲁棒性基准：覆盖三种模型族（逻辑回归、前馈神经网络、表格Transformer）与四种仅限申请人可修改特征的攻击（FGSM、PGD、椒盐噪声S&P、DeepFool），并设置混合攻击场景；采用分层交叉验证在全网格上评估。

Result: 对抗训练能显著提升对其所训练攻击的鲁棒性，并在基于梯度的攻击族内迁移良好，但对非梯度型破坏迁移较弱；单一攻击防御会高估真实世界鲁棒性；混合训练在异质攻击下提供最均衡的鲁棒性，同时保持干净测试集性能。

Conclusion: 单一攻击防御不足以反映真实鲁棒性，信用模型治理应支持多攻击压力测试；混合训练是兼顾异质攻击鲁棒性与干净性能的可行策略。

Abstract: Machine learning-based credit scoring is increasingly central to Peer-to-Peer (P2P) lending, yet its resilience to adversarial manipulation, where applicants strategically alter self-reported inputs to secure favourable decisions, remains poorly understood. Most adversarial-robustness evidence comes from image and text domains and evaluates a single attack against a matching defence, offering little guidance on how defences generalise across attack types in tabular credit data. We address this with a systematic train-test robustness benchmark on a large Lending Club subset, spanning three model families (logistic regression, a feed-forward neural network, and a transformer for tabular data) and four attacks confined to applicant-mutable features: Fast Gradient Sign Method (FGSM), Projected Gradient Descent (PGD), Salt-and-Pepper (S&P) noise, and DeepFool, plus a mixed-attack regime. Across a full grid evaluated with stratified cross-validation, adversarial training sharply improves robustness against the attack it is trained on and transfers well within the gradient-based family, but transfers weakly to non-gradient corruption, so single-attack defences overstate real-world resilience. Mixed training delivers the most balanced robustness across heterogeneous attacks while preserving clean-test performance, supporting multi-attack stress testing in credit-model governance.

</details>


### [253] [Deep Neural Networks for Learning Intent from sEMG Signals to Support Hardware Devices for Post-Stroke Neurorehabilitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09971)
*Zakariyya Brewster,Divy Wadhwani,Emily Yan,Aidan Wang,Karma Namgyal,Shuting Xie,Markiyan Konyk,Tala Abdelmaguid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 构建PhysioMio卒中患者高密度sEMG五指令解码流水线，比较LSTM/CNN/GNN，经知识蒸馏压缩至四通道123K参数模型并导出ONNX。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卒中后残余肌电仍可测，指特异性运动意图是神经康复的有价值控制信号，需要可复现、可嵌入的软件通路。

Method: 用PhysioMio双侧纵向数据集；对齐标签、20–450 Hz Butterworth滤波与Symlet-4小波去噪、200 ms重叠窗分段、每通道12个时频特征；对比LSTM、CNN、GNN基线并做架构搜索；按ECRB、ECRL、FDS、FDP四通道重训CNN-Micro，排除地电极，采用跨通道知识蒸馏。

Result: LSTM子集准确率最高0.545；GNN宏F1最高0.706、宏AUPRC 0.776；CNN-Large单划分最佳0.593子集准确率、0.714宏F1。四通道CNN-Micro五种子：子集准确率0.5219±0.0114、手指准确率0.7612±0.0038、宏F1 0.6095±0.0058。

Conclusion: 建立了从卒中后sEMG到紧凑五指意图预测的可复现软件路径，123K参数模型接收9窗×48特征并已导出ONNX，供后续硬件在环评估。

Abstract: Finger-specific motor intent is a clinically meaningful control signal for post-stroke neurorehabilitation, where residual muscle activity may remain measurable despite weak or incomplete movement. We study five-finger multilabel intent decoding from impaired-arm high-density surface electromyography (sEMG) in PhysioMio, a bilateral longitudinal dataset collected from stroke patients. A common processing protocol aligns movement labels, applies 20--450 Hz Butterworth filtering and Symlet-4 wavelet denoising, segments overlapping 200 ms windows, and extracts twelve time- and frequency-domain descriptors per channel. Direct LSTM, CNN, and GNN baselines reveal complementary behavior: the LSTM attains the highest subset accuracy (0.545), whereas the GNN attains the highest macro F1 (0.706) and macro AUPRC (0.776). Architecture search then identifies CNN-Large as the strongest single-split CNN, with 0.593 subset accuracy and 0.714 macro F1, while CNN-Micro provides a compact architecture for embedded inference. To match a four-sensor hardware design, we retrain CNN-Micro using channels associated with ECRB, ECRL, FDS, and FDP and exclude the ground electrode from model input. Across five seeds, cross-channel knowledge distillation improves the four-channel student over direct training, reaching $0.5219 \pm 0.0114$ subset accuracy, $0.7612 \pm 0.0038$ finger accuracy, and $0.6095 \pm 0.0058$ macro F1. The selected 123K-parameter model accepts nine windows of 48 features and has been exported to ONNX. These results establish a reproducible software path from post-stroke sEMG to compact five-finger intent prediction for subsequent hardware-in-the-loop evaluation.

</details>


### [254] [An Explainable Machine Learning Framework for Predicting Blood-Brain Barrier Permeability Using Molecular Descriptors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10012)
*Fatemeh Mahmoudi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究构建了基于分子描述子的可解释机器学习框架，用于预测血脑屏障渗透性；优化后的XGBoost模型表现最优（准确率88.97%，ROC-AUC 0.9282），SHAP分析显示TPSA、HBD和LogP为关键描述符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血脑屏障渗透性是中枢神经系统药物研发中的关键决定因素，直接影响候选药物能否到达脑内靶点。因此，早期准确预测BBB渗透性对CNS药物筛选具有重要意义。

Method: 基于MoleculeNet BBBP数据集，使用RDKit生成分子描述子，提取2,039个化合物的15个理化描述符；训练Logistic Regression、SVM、Random Forest和XGBoost四种监督学习算法；采用GridSearchCV进行超参数优化，并使用SHAP进行模型可解释性分析。

Result: 优化后的XGBoost分类器性能最佳：准确率88.97%、精确率88.92%、召回率97.76%、F1分数93.13%、ROC-AUC 0.9282；分层五折交叉验证平均ROC-AUC为0.8982 ± 0.0130；特征重要性和SHAP分析一致表明TPSA、HBD和LogP是影响BBB渗透性预测的最重要分子描述符。

Conclusion: 该框架为BBB渗透性预测提供了一种准确、可解释且计算高效的方法，可作为中枢神经系统候选药物早期筛选的有价值工具。

Abstract: Blood-brain barrier (BBB) permeability is a critical determinant in the development of central nervous system therapeutics because it directly influences the ability of drug candidates to reach their target sites within the brain. In this study, an explainable machine learning framework was developed to predict BBB permeability using molecular descriptors generated from the MoleculeNet BBBP dataset with the RDKit cheminformatics toolkit. Fifteen physicochemical descriptors extracted from 2,039 compounds were used to train four supervised machine learning algorithms, including Logistic Regression, Support Vector Machine (SVM), Random Forest, and Extreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost). Hyperparameter optimization was performed using GridSearchCV, while model interpretability was investigated using SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP). Among the evaluated models, the optimized XGBoost classifier achieved the best predictive performance, with an accuracy of 88.97%, a precision of 88.92%, a recall of 97.76%, an F1-score of 93.13%, and a ROC-AUC of 0.9282. Stratified five-fold cross-validation further demonstrated the robustness of the proposed model, yielding a mean ROC-AUC of 0.8982 +/- 0.0130. Feature importance and SHAP analyses consistently identified TPSA, HBD, and LogP as the most influential molecular descriptors governing BBB permeability prediction. Overall, the proposed framework provides an accurate, interpretable, and computationally efficient approach for BBB permeability prediction and may serve as a valuable tool for the early-stage screening of CNS drug candidates.

</details>


### [255] [MetroLLM-Bench: Evaluating Language Models as Transit Kiosk Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10016)
*Remco Hendriks*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MetroLLM-Bench，一个包含955个案例的地铁服务终端策略层基准，覆盖6个真实地铁系统与11类任务；通过Tier 1确定性评分与Tier 2语义评分评估26个模型，发现经PEFT微调的4B Qwen 3.5学生模型在Tier 1上超越更大规模闭源模型，且PEFT增益随基座规模增大而递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型评测多集中于通用问答或代码任务，缺乏对"作为交通服务终端策略层"这一具身化、工具调用与结构化输出场景的系统性检验。该场景要求模型在真实地铁网络规模（37–414站）下完成路由、票价计算、故障应对、无障碍与对抗输入处理，并输出机器可渲染的终端状态，因此需要一个兼具确定性与语义质量维度的专用基准，以衡量模型在受约束业务流程中的可靠性及可部署性。

Method: 构建MetroLLM-Bench，含955个案例，覆盖6个真实地铁系统与11类任务（路由、票价计算、中断、无障碍、对抗输入等）。每个案例要求模型调用结构化工具并提交机器可渲染终端状态，包含outcome、适用时的单票票价报价以及kiosk action。评分分两层：Tier 1由14个确定性组件构成，Tier 2由8个语义质量组件构成（其中6个使用语言模型裁判），报告Tier 1与两层合并得分。采用分层75/25划分，717例用于训练数据生成，238例用于留出评估。评估来自6家厂商的26个模型（23个参与排名），并对2B/4B/9B/27B Qwen系列以三种随机种子进行PEFT与基座模型对比，另设确定性规则基线。

Result: 留出集上，经PEFT训练的4B Qwen 3.5学生模型Tier 1得91.3，超过GPT-5.6两档（90.6和90.0），与满推理强度下的GPT-5.4持平（91.4），模型体积仅2.6 GB（Q4_K_M）。9B与27B学生模型在该训练规模下未带来进一步Tier 1提升。四个Qwen规模上，PEFT相对基座模型的增益从2B的+7.03分降至27B的-0.91分，且每个规模下全部种子方向一致。确定性规则基线Tier 1达84.6，语言模型剩余优势集中于策略适配、复合场景、无障碍与时间推理。Muse Glimmer 30B在综合排名中领先，仅服务配置差异即使Qwen 3.5与3.8的比较在Tier 1上移动2.7分。

Conclusion: MetroLLM-Bench表明，在小规模PEFT下，小参数模型（4B）即可在受约束的交通终端策略任务上媲美甚至超越前沿闭源大模型，且具备低资源部署优势；但PEFT收益随基座规模增大而消失甚至转为负值，提示训练规模与模型容量间存在匹配边界。评测同时显示确定性规则基线已具较强实力，语言模型价值主要体现在策略适配与复杂语义场景。基准、评测框架、复现指南与微调学生模型已开源。

Abstract: We introduce MetroLLM-Bench, a 955-case benchmark for testing language models as the policy layer of a transit kiosk. It covers six real metro systems, ranging from 37 to 414 stations, and eleven categories that include routing, fare calculation, disruptions, accessibility, and adversarial input. In each case, the model must call structured tools and submit a machine-renderable terminal state containing an outcome, a per-ticket fare quote when applicable, and a kiosk action. Fourteen deterministic scoring components form Tier 1; eight semantic-quality components form Tier 2, six of which use a language-model judge. We report Tier 1 and the combined score of both tiers. A stratified 75/25 split reserves 717 cases for training-data generation and 238 for held-out evaluation.
  We evaluate twenty-six models from six vendors, of which twenty-three are ranked. On the held-out partition, a 4B Qwen 3.5 student trained through parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) exceeds both GPT-5.6 tiers on Tier 1 (91.3 against 90.6 and 90.0) and matches GPT-5.4 full at maximum reasoning effort (91.4), with a 2.6 GB Q4_K_M footprint. Larger 9B and 27B students provide no further Tier 1 improvement over the 4B student at this training scale. Across the four Qwen sizes, the PEFT gain over the corresponding base model decreases from +7.03 points at 2B (three training seeds) to -0.91 at 27B; every seed shows the same direction at every size. A deterministic rule-based baseline reaches 84.6 on Tier 1, with the remaining language-model advantage concentrated in policy adaptation, compound scenarios, accessibility, and temporal reasoning. Muse Glimmer 30B leads the composite ranking, and serving configuration alone moves the Qwen 3.5-to-3.8 comparison by 2.7 Tier 1 points. The benchmark, harness, reproduction guide, and fine-tuned students are released at https://github.com/continker/metrollm-bench.

</details>


### [256] [Structure-Aware Unsupervised Anomaly Detection for Spacecraft Telemetry with Adaptive EVT Thresholding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10017)
*Óscar Alcarria,Rafael Sánchez,Javier Sempere,Pablo Torrijos,Juan C. Alfaro,Juan M. Auñón,José A. Gámez,José M. Puerta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无监督、可部署的航天器遥测异常检测框架，无需标签、先验故障知识或任务特定调参，从运行第二个月即可预测；结合逐月增量重训练、统计模型选择与自适应EVT阈值控制误报，在ESA-AD上严格时序评估取得F0.5=0.700（任务1）与0.698（任务2）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航天器遥测运行异常检测通常依赖有标注的历史异常数据或较长预热期，而实际场景中这些条件难以满足，因此需要一种无需标签与先验知识、可快速部署的无监督方案。

Method: 框架融合三部分：逐月增量重训练、统计模型选择，以及基于自适应极值理论（EVT）的阈值设定以控制误报。

Result: 在ESA Anomalies Dataset（ESA-AD）上采用严格时序评估：任务1的F0.5=0.700，任务2的F0.5=0.698。

Conclusion: 该无监督框架不依赖标签、先验故障知识或任务特定调优，从运行第二个月起即可产出预测，具备实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Operational anomaly detection in spacecraft telemetry typically requires labeled historical anomalies or extended warm-up periods. These requirements are rarely met in practice. We propose an unsupervised, deployment-ready framework that produces predictions from the second month of operation without any labels, prior fault knowledge, or mission-specific tuning. The approach combines incremental monthly retraining, statistical model selection, and adaptive Extreme Value Theory (EVT) thresholding for false alarm control. On the ESA Anomalies Dataset (ESA-AD), it achieves $F_{0.5}=0.700$ on Mission~1 and $F_{0.5}=0.698$ on Mission~2 under strict chronological evaluation.

</details>


### [257] [Beyond Contact Sensors: Deep learning with Pseudo-Labeling for remote Photoplethysmography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10026)
*Bhargav Acharya,Barbara Hammer,Hanna Drimalla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了在rPPG任务中，用无监督信号处理方法生成的伪标签替代接触式传感器标签训练深度学习模型的可行性。结果表明：同步不佳的数据集上伪标签训练优于监督训练；同步良好时结果混合，且标签质量（如异常参与者）是关键影响因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习rPPG方法依赖需要精确视频-真值同步的标注数据集，而此类数据采集费时费力；相比之下经典信号处理方法无需此类标签。因此作者希望探究在何种条件下，无监督信号处理生成的伪标签可替代接触式传感器标签用于训练深度学习模型，从而降低数据采集负担。

Method: 采用系统化评估，对比伪标签训练与基于接触式传感器标签的监督训练。评估涵盖同步不完美的数据集与同步良好的数据集，并在同数据集内评估与跨数据集评估两种设置下进行；同时分析标签质量的影响（如剔除单个异常参与者）。

Result: 对于同步不完美的数据集，伪标签方法优于基于接触式传感器的监督训练；对于同步良好的数据集，结果混合：同数据集评估中两种训练方式无显著差异，而跨数据集评估更倾向于监督训练；但剔除单个异常参与者后，伪标签方法的跨数据集性能显著提升。

Conclusion: 信号处理方法可为深度学习模型生成有效的训练信号，在保持竞争力性能的同时减少对高成本数据采集的依赖；同时标签质量对伪标签方法的性能至关重要。

Abstract: Heart rate is a critical biomarker of health, and remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) enables its contactless estimation from video data for telemedicine applications. Recent advancements in deep learning based rPPG methods achieve state-of-the-art results, outperforming classical signal-processing methods in complex scenarios. However, deep learning methods depend on datasets with precise synchronization between videos and ground truth signals collected via contact sensors, whereas signal-processing-based methods do not. To address this dependence on labeled datasets, which are labor-intensive to collect, we investigate under which circumstances pseudo-labels extracted using unsupervised signal-processing methods can replace contact sensors labels for training deep learning methods. Our systematic evaluations found that for datasets with imperfect synchronization, the pseudo-label approach outperforms supervised training on contact sensors. For datasets with good synchronization, results are mixed: within-dataset evaluation shows no significant difference between training methods, while cross-dataset evaluation favors supervised training. However, removing a single outlier participant significantly improves the pseudo-label approach's cross-dataset performance, highlighting the importance of label quality. These results demonstrate that signal-processing methods can generate valid training signals for deep learning models, reducing dependency on labor-intensive dataset collection while maintaining competitive performance.

</details>


### [258] [Field-level prediction of mid-plane stress tensor fields in concrete target penetration: a cross-velocity graph neural operator surrogate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10032)
*Wenpu Du,Peng Zhou,Yunlong Xia,Sinuo Xin,Congcong Zhang,Boyang Zhang,Yi Zhang,Wenzheng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文建立"细观非均质—全场应力张量预测"框架：用全尺寸骨料显式LS-DYNA模型生成400例（4速度×100骨料随机种子）X-Z中面六分量应力场数据，经终端侵彻状态逐例验证界定可靠观测量，训练场级图神经验算子代理模型并做跨速度留一外推，形成可复现数据库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混凝土抗侵彻已有大量研究，但缺乏把细观尺度非均质性（骨料随机分布）与全场六分量应力张量预测联系起来的统一框架；且数值模拟中终端侵彻状态与深度是否为静止态常未经检验。

Method: 全尺寸骨料显式LS-DYNA模拟（弹径45 mm、质量2.13 kg，靶径500 mm×厚200 mm，网格10 mm），依构型相似性与Frew 2006、Hanchak 1992、Forrestal 1996已发表侵彻实验对照验证；输出X-Z中面六分量应力张量场，共400例（4种撞击速度×100个骨料随机种子）；对终端侵彻状态逐例核验；构建场级图神经验算子（GNO）代理模型学习应力场时变演化，并做跨速度留一法外推评估。

Result: 100、135、200 m/s工况在计算窗口结束时仍在运动（速度为负，即回弹），仅1例165 m/s工况停止；故侵彻深度除该唯一停止工况（69.33 mm）外不作为静止态标量报告，且证实弹头节点深度差异为侵蚀后位移积分的数值伪影。代理模型单步相对L2误差0.6977（如实报告）；自回归滚动预测第11至39帧约耗时144 ms，相对单核LS-DYNA加速约3.6×10^3~4.3×10^3倍。

Conclusion: 三项贡献为：终端侵彻状态逐例验证将可靠观测量锚定于刚体运动与场级应力演化；场级图神经验算子实现应力场时空预测与跨速度外推；建立全尺寸、骨料显式、跨速度、逐种子的可复现数据库。其验证受构型相似性与场级自一致性的边界限制，属所研参数空间内基于模拟训练的决策支持方法而非实验替代。

Abstract: Although the impact resistance of concrete has been studied extensively, a framework linking mesoscale heterogeneity to full-field stress-tensor prediction has been lacking. Data were generated with a full-scale aggregate-resolved LS-DYNA model (projectile diameter 45 mm, mass 2.13 kg, target diameter 500 mm x thickness 200 mm, mesh 10 mm), verified against published penetration experiments (Frew 2006, Hanchak 1992, Forrestal 1996) by configuration similarity. The dataset contains six-component stress-tensor fields on the X-Z mid-plane for 400 cases (4 impact velocities x 100 aggregate seeds). Three contributions are reported. First, case-by-case verification of the terminal penetration state delimited the rest-state validity of penetration depth and anchored reliable observables to rigid-body motion and field-level stress evolution. Second, a field-level graph neural operator surrogate learned the time-varying stress-field evolution and evaluated cross-velocity leave-one-out extrapolation. Third, the full-scale, aggregate-resolved, cross-velocity, per-seed database was established as a reproducible resource. Cases at 100, 135 and 200 m/s still moved at window end (negative velocity, i.e. rebound), and only one 165 m/s case arrested. Penetration depth is therefore not reported as a rest-state scalar except for the single arrested case (69.33 mm); nose-node depth differences were confirmed as numerical artifacts of displacement integration after erosion. The single-step relative L2 error was 0.6977, reported honestly; autoregressive rollout from frame 11 to 39 took about 144 ms, a speedup of about 3.6x10^3 to 4.3x10^3 relative to single-core LS-DYNA, reported as application value. Validation is bounded by configuration similarity and field-level self-consistency; the framework is a simulation-trained decision-support method within the studied parameter space.

</details>


### [259] [Hybrid Quantum-Classical NLP Classification with Compact Semantic Representations: An Experimental Analysis of Representation Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10089)
*Ali Hassan,Zijia Zhao,Maha A. Metawei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究高维句嵌入到低维表示的降维流程，用于变分量子分类，发现监督式降维（LDA/NCA）在5维时即可达到接近全维经典基线的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型与句嵌入模型能提供丰富语义表示，但其高维性超出近期量子机器学习（QML）电路可处理的输入特征数量，因此需要将高维句嵌入压缩为紧凑表示以适配变分量子分类器。

Method: 构建混合量子-经典流水线：预训练句嵌入模型 → 降维 → 角度编码 → 变分量子电路（VQC）→ 经典决策层。系统比较PCA（无监督）、NCA与LDA（监督）三种降维方法，在TREC问题分类数据集上考察表示维度、信息保留率、量子比特数与分类性能之间的关系，并采用无泄漏交叉验证协议。

Result: PCA存在严重信息瓶颈：将768维嵌入降至3、4、5、8维仅保留约8.2%、10.2%、11.9%、16.4%的方差，对应准确率为50.3%、51.2%、57.9%、63.4%。监督式降维效率显著更高：仅用5维时LDA达85.3%、NCA达83.1%，而384维全维经典基线为85.1%。

Conclusion: 监督式降维比基于方差的压缩能更有效地保留任务相关信息，紧凑表示是通向实用混合量子-经典NLP模型的有前景路径。

Abstract: Large language and sentence-embedding models provide rich semantic representations, but their high dimensionality poses a challenge for near-term quantum machine learning (QML), where quantum circuits can process only a limited number of input features. We investigate a hybrid quantum-classical pipeline that transforms high-dimensional sentence embeddings into compact representations for variational quantum classification. The workflow combines a pretrained sentence-embedding model, dimensionality reduction, angle encoding, a variational quantum circuit (VQC), and a classical decision layer. We systematically compare principal component analysis (PCA), neighborhood components analysis (NCA), and linear discriminant analysis (LDA), covering both unsupervised and supervised dimensionality reduction. Using the TREC question-classification dataset, we study the relationship between representation dimensionality, information retention, qubit count, and classification performance. Preliminary PCA experiments reveal a strong information bottleneck: reducing 768-dimensional embeddings to 3, 4, 5, and 8 dimensions retains about 8.2%, 10.2%, 11.9%, and 16.4% of the variance, with corresponding classification accuracies of 50.3%, 51.2%, 57.9%, and 63.4%. In contrast, supervised reduction is substantially more efficient. LDA reaches 85.3% accuracy and NCA reaches 83.1% using only 5 dimensions, under a leakage-free cross-validation protocol, compared with 85.1% for a full 384-dimensional classical baseline. These results indicate that supervised dimensionality reduction can preserve task-relevant information far more effectively than variance-based compression, making compact representations a promising route toward practical hybrid quantum-classical NLP models.

</details>


### [260] [A Systematic Evaluation of Molecule Generation Models for De Novo Drug Design: From Benchmarks to Practical Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10099)
*Xinrui Xu,Xueer Wang,Dan Luo,Sisi Yuan,Xuan Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了面向从头药物设计的分子生成模型，覆盖82种方法、五类深度生成框架，系统整合了基准、分子表示、评估指标与实验验证案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子生成已成为从头药物设计的强大计算工具，但现有综述多孤立讨论特定模型家族或应用场景，缺乏对生成流程各组成部分如何协同形成完整工作流的整合视角。

Method: 综述性研究：梳理五类深度生成框架（RNN/Transformer、VAE、GAN、流模型、扩散模型）共82种方法；总结常用基准与分子表示；分析通用生成与口袋条件生成的原理；对常用基准和评估指标下的报道性能进行系统综合与比较分析，并总结代表性实验验证案例。

Result: 提供了跨五类生成框架、82种方法的系统性能对比分析，整合了基准资源、评估指标与模型参考，并纳入代表性实验验证案例；相关资源公开于GitHub仓库。

Conclusion: 未来方向包括标准化3D数据、相互作用感知生成、受体柔性和多目标分子设计，以提升分子生成的可靠性与实验相关性。

Abstract: Molecule generation has emerged as a powerful computational tool for de novo drug design, enabling the exploration of chemical space beyond the limits of conventional virtual screening. The field has progressed rapidly, driven by advances in molecular representations, generative architectures, and target-aware modeling strategies. However, existing reviews typically address specific model families or application scenarios in isolation, rather than offering an integrated perspective on how these components collectively form a coherent generation workflow. In this review, we present a comprehensive evaluation of molecule generation models for de novo drug design, covering 82 methods across five deep generative frameworks, including recurrent neural network (RNN)- and Transformer-based models, variational autoencoders (VAEs), generative adversarial networks (GANs), flow-based models, and diffusion models. We first summarize widely used benchmarks and molecular representations, and then examine the methodological principles underlying both general and pocket-conditioned generation. A central contribution of this work is a systematic synthesis and comparative analysis of reported performance across commonly used benchmarks and evaluation metrics. We also summarize representative experimentally validated case studies. Looking ahead, we discuss future directions in standardized 3D data, interaction-aware generation, receptor flexibility, and multi-objective molecular design, with the aim of improving the reliability and experimental relevance of molecule generation. All collected benchmark resources, evaluation metrics, and model references are provided in a publicly accessible repository at https://github.com/JacklinGroup/molecule-generation-review.

</details>


### [261] [A Trust-Network-Based Federated Learning Framework for Multi-Center Aging Clock Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10108)
*Chunxu Zhang,Bo Li,Wenliang Wang,Yang Liu,Di Jiang,Yuan Huang,Yo-ichi Nabeshima,Akinori Yamamura,Bo Yang,Qiang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TNFL——基于信任网络的联邦学习框架，融合年龄感知混合专家与生成式回放，在隐私约束下的分布式分子数据中实现可解释的衰老时钟预测，并揭示衰老相关的高阶蛋白互作组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 衰老时钟可量化生物学衰老并刻画个体健康状态，但厘清哪些蛋白互作（零阶或高阶）对精确衰老时钟重要，需要从跨医疗中心的大规模分子数据中学习。隐私约束阻碍集中式数据共享，联邦学习成为自然选择，但该场景面临四类挑战：本地样本量有限、中心间信任稀疏且有方向性、需在支持可解释性的同时保持判别性年龄预测、以及异构跨中心数据下的模型漂移与遗忘。

Method: 提出TNFL（Trust-Network-based Federated Learning）：不依赖中心化聚合，沿有向成对信任关系渐进式传播模型；将年龄感知的混合专家模型（age-aware mixture-of-experts）与生成式回放（generative replay）相结合，以保留已学信息、缓解遗忘与漂移。

Result: 在多个分子数据集上，TNFL在本地数据有限时仍能有效进行衰老时钟预测，提供可解释的年龄相关预测模式，并在不同交互阶数下保持稳定性能。对TNFL识别出的成对蛋白互作及其高阶组织进行功能与网络分析显示：这些互作反复形成跨越多个衰老相关生物系统的协同高阶子网络，且若干蛋白在多个子网络中重复出现。

Conclusion: TNFL不仅解决了隐私约束下分布式分子数据中的联邦学习挑战，还能捕捉超越孤立成对关联的分子关系，揭示与衰老相关的连贯高阶生物学组织，表明高阶蛋白互作对精确衰老时钟具有重要意义。

Abstract: Aging clocks quantify biological aging and help characterize individual health status. What protein interactions are important for accurate aging clocks, and are they zeroth-order or higher-order? Addressing these questions requires learning from large molecular datasets distributed across medical centers, where privacy constraints prevent centralized data sharing. Federated learning offers a natural solution but faces four challenges in this setting: limited local sample sizes, sparse and directional inter-center trust, the need to retain discriminative age prediction while supporting interpretation, and model drift and forgetting under heterogeneous cross-center data.
  We propose TNFL, a trust-network-based federated learning framework that progressively propagates models along directed pairwise trust relations without centralized aggregation. TNFL combines an age-aware mixture-of-experts model with generative replay to preserve previously learned information and reduce forgetting and drift. Experiments across multiple molecular datasets show that TNFL enables effective aging-clock prediction with limited local data, provides interpretable age-dependent prediction patterns, and maintains stable performance across interaction orders.
  To investigate the biological questions, we analyze TNFL-identified pairwise protein interactions and their higher-order organization through functional and network analyses. The identified interactions repeatedly form coordinated higher-order subnetworks spanning multiple aging-related biological systems, with several proteins recurring across subnetworks. These findings suggest that TNFL captures molecular relationships beyond isolated pairwise associations and reveals coherent higher-order biological organization associated with aging.

</details>


### [262] [Storage-Scalable Progressive Semantic Communication via Knowledge-Base Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10112)
*Heng Zhu,Ye Liu,Kun Zhu,Feifei Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出存储可扩展的知识库复用量化（SSKBQ），通过复用紧凑KB集合解耦传输阶段数与知识库数量，缓解存储随传输深度线性增长的问题，并保持渐进式重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识库辅助语义通信中，单知识库量化（SKBQ）存储开销小但量化能力有限；多知识库残差量化（MKBQ）虽支持逐级细化，却因每阶段独立分配知识库导致KB存储随传输深度线性增长，存在存储可扩展性问题。

Method: 提出存储可扩展知识库复用量化（SSKBQ）：在多个残差细化阶段复用一组紧凑知识库，使传输阶段数与维护的KB数量解耦；并引入阶段感知残差监督机制，对中间量化表示进行正则化，促进渐进式细化。

Result: 实验结果表明，知识库复用能够有效解决存储可扩展性问题，同时保持具有竞争力的渐进式重建性能。

Conclusion: SSKBQ以KB复用替代逐阶段独立KB分配，在显著改善存储可扩展性的同时维持渐进式重建质量，为知识库辅助语义通信提供了一种有效的存储效率方案。

Abstract: Existing knowledge-base-assisted semantic communication schemes commonly adopt either single knowledge-base quantization (SKBQ) or multi-knowledge-base residual quantization (MKBQ). SKBQ incurs limited storage overhead but has restricted quantization capacity, whereas MKBQ supports progressive refinement by assigning an independent knowledge base (KB) to each stage, causing the KB storage to grow linearly with the transmission depth. To address this problem, we propose storage-scalable knowledge-base reuse quantization (SSKBQ), which reuses a compact set of KBs across multiple residual refinement stages and thereby decouples the number of transmission stages from the number of maintained KBs. A stage-aware residual supervision mechanism is further introduced to regularize intermediate quantized representations and encourage progressive refinement. Experimental results demonstrate that KB reuse provides an effective solution to the storage scalability problem while maintaining competitive progressive reconstruction performance.

</details>


### [263] [CompassOPD: Cross-Family On-Policy Distillation via Within-Family Likelihood Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10154)
*Naibin Gu,Qingyi Si,Chenxu Yang,Chuanyu Qin,Junhao Zhou,Peng Fu,Zheng Lin,Weiping Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现跨模型家族的在策略蒸馏（OPD）效果退化，其原因是学生-教师间的“偏移”主导了更新方向；提出 CompassOPD，通过去除偏移、仅迁移强教师相对同家族弱参考的似然变化来恢复蒸馏收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD 能对学生自生成轨迹提供密集的 token 级监督，在同家族（teacher 与 student 同源）时表现很好。但在跨家族场景下，即使做了 tokenizer 对齐，效果仍明显下降，且更强的外部教师几乎带不来额外提升。作者希望解释这种“教师变强但收益不增”的断连现象，并恢复跨家族 OPD 的有效性。

Method: 将跨家族 OPD 信号分解为两部分：(1) 低能力“教师家族参考模型”与学生之间的 offset（偏移）；(2) 从该参考到强教师的 within-family log-likelihood shift（家族内似然变化）。标准 OPD 把两者一起迁移，导致 offset 主导更新方向，掩盖了真正与教师能力提升相关的信号。CompassOPD 的做法是移除 offset、只迁移 within-family shift，并用一个冻结的学生参考把更新锚定在学生初始策略上，使教师侧与学生侧的变化都在各自家族内部度量。对 MoE 教师，进一步通过降低专家激活从教师 checkpoint 直接构造参考，无需额外参考 checkpoint。

Result: 在三个学生家族、多个教师家族上的实验表明，CompassOPD 一致优于标准跨家族 OPD，平均推理准确率最高提升 5.50 分。对 MoE 教师，用降专家激活方式从教师 checkpoint 现场构造参考，仍比 OPD 高 3.43 分。

Conclusion: 跨家族 OPD 的瓶颈来自家族间“偏移”对更新方向的污染，而非教师能力不足；把监督重构为家族内相对变化并用冻结学生锚定，可显著提升跨家族蒸馏效果，且对 MoE 教师能省去独立参考 checkpoint。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense token-level supervision on student-generated trajectories. Although OPD performs strongly when teacher and student belong to the same model family, we find that its effectiveness degrades in cross-family settings even after tokenizer alignment, with substantially stronger external teachers offering little additional improvement. To understand this disconnect, we decompose the cross-family OPD signal into two components: an offset between a low-capability teacher-family reference and the student, and the within-family log-likelihood shift from that reference to the strong teacher. Standard OPD transfers both components together, allowing the offset to dominate the update direction and obscure the changes associated with teacher capability improvements. We propose CompassOPD, which removes this offset and transfers the within-family shift, while a frozen student reference anchors updates to the student's initial policy. Thus, both teacher-side and student-side changes are measured within their respective model families. Experiments across three student families and multiple teacher families show that CompassOPD consistently outperforms standard cross-family OPD, improving average reasoning accuracy by up to 5.50 points. For an MoE teacher, we further construct the reference directly from the teacher checkpoint by reducing expert activation, eliminating the need for a separate reference checkpoint while retaining a 3.43-point gain over OPD.

</details>


### [264] [CoGe-GCD: Reframing Generalized Category Discovery with Compositional Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10158)
*Luyao Tang,Jiewei Zheng,Kunze Huang,Chaoqi Chen,Yue Huang,Cheng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出CoGe-GCD，通过组合泛化视角重构广义类别发现（GCD），在骨干网络与投影头之间插入归纳偏置模块，显著提升已知/新类划分与未知类别数估计等性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GCD方法基于无结构的token特征，难以对新类别的"新组合"进行外推，缺乏类似人类的组合推理能力（重用已知类学到的原语并判断新组合是否构成新类别）。

Method: 提出两阶段耦合框架CoGe-GCD：(i) 组合感知（Compositional Perception）：将patch token映射到原语词表，通过竞争性token-原语分配与信息传递精炼token嵌入，形成连贯的分组用于发现；(ii) 泛化归纳（Generalizing Induction）：利用诱导出的几何结构，在空间关系上做结构保持校准，在维持概率语义的同时提升对未见原语组合的外推。该模块作为归纳偏置插入骨干与投影头之间，不改变头部与损失函数，可即插即用。

Result: 在标准基准上，一致提升全类准确率、未知类数量估计与几何质量，且计算开销极小。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 将GCD重新表述为组合泛化问题，并给出可插拔的通用模块，验证了以组合原语与几何校准驱动新类别发现的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Generalized Category Discovery (GCD) assigns unlabeled instances, mixed with labeled data, to known or novel categories, requiring human-like compositional reasoning: reusing primitives learned from known classes and deciding when new combinations imply new categories. Existing GCD methods operate on unstructured token features and struggle to extrapolate to novel compositions. We propose CoGe-GCD, which rethinks GCD through compositional generalization with two coupled stages. (i) Compositional Perception structures patch tokens by mapping them to a small vocabulary of primitives and refining token embeddings via competitive token-primitive assignment and information passing, yielding coherent groups for discovery. (ii) Generalizing Induction exploits the induced geometric structure and applies a structure-preserving calibration over spatial relations, maintaining probabilistic semantics while improving extrapolation to unseen primitive combinations. CoGe-GCD is implemented as an inductive-bias module between backbone and projection head, without modifying heads or losses, and can be plugged into diverse GCD frameworks. On standard benchmarks, it consistently improves all-class accuracy, unknown-class number estimation, and geometric quality, with marginal computational overhead. Code is available at https://github.com/lytang63/CoGe-GCD.

</details>


### [265] [An Exponential Deterministic--Randomized Gap in ERM-Oracle Complexity for Thresholds on an Unknown Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10196)
*Xuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了通过一致性型 ERM oracle 访问概念类的 transductive online learning of thresholds on an unknown total order，证明了随机化能否减少 oracle 调用次数取决于 oracle 的选择规则，而非概念类本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Attias, Hanneke 和 Ramaswami (NeurIPS 2025) 提出随机化是否可证明地减少仅通过 oracle 访问概念类时的 oracle 调用次数。本文针对其特别指出的实例——未知全序上 T 个实例的 transductive online learning of thresholds——探究该问题，以确定随机化与确定性学习器之间的分离及其成因。

Method: 作者研究带一致性型 ERM oracle 的阈值学习问题，主要分析 minimal-prefix 规则和 maximal-prefix 规则：证明确定性学习器在 M+Q≥T-ε 下界；构造显式困难分布给出随机学习器的期望错误下界；并对比 feasible-median、global-median 及对抗性冻结的 memoryless oracle 等不同 oracle 选择规则，得出相应分离结论，同时给出固定查询预算的部分权衡结果以及弱一致性 oracle 接口对比。

Result: 对 minimal-prefix（或 maximal-prefix）固定 oracle，每个确定性学习器在某实例上满足 M+Q≥T-ε（ε∈{0,1}），常数精确；O(log T) 错误需 T-ε-O(log T) 次调用，而随机学习器在相同规则下可达 O(log T) 期望调用与错误。随机界最优：在 minimal-prefix 下的显式困难分布上，任意学习器期望错误至少为 ((T+1-ε)128^{-E[Q]}-1)/2，故多对数错误需 Ω(log T) 期望调用。分离由 oracle 选择规则决定：legal feasible-median 规则下确定性学习器可达 O(log T) 调用与错误，global-median 规则仍导致线性总成本；对抗性选择并冻结的 memoryless oracle 同样给出线性界。固定查询预算下给出部分权衡（中间区间未解决）；仅弱一致性 oracle（返回 realizability 位）时，确定性与随机学习器均需 Θ(T) 次调用。

Conclusion: 随机化对 oracle 调用次数的优势严格依赖于 oracle 的选择规则而非概念类本身：在 minimal/maximal-prefix 及 global-median 规则下存在精确分离或线性下界，而 feasible-median 规则下随机化并无优势。同时，固定查询预算的中间权衡区间仍开放，且弱一致性 oracle 接口下随机化无法改善 Θ(T) 的调用复杂度。

Abstract: Attias, Hanneke and Ramaswami (NeurIPS 2025) asked whether randomization provably reduces the oracle calls needed for online learning when the class is accessible only through an oracle. We study the instance they singled out: transductive online learning of thresholds on an unknown total order of T instances, with a consistency-type ERM oracle that returns a full concept consistent with a queried labeled set (or reports non-realizability). Our main result is a separation for a fixed natural oracle. When the oracle is the minimal-prefix rule (or the maximal-prefix rule), every deterministic learner makes M mistakes and Q calls with $M+Q\ge T-\varepsilon$ on some instance ($\varepsilon\in\{0,1\}$, according to whether the empty prefix is a concept), and the constant is exact; hence $O(\log T)$ mistakes cost $T-\varepsilon-O(\log T)$ calls, whereas that paper's randomized learner achieves $O(\log T)$ expected calls and mistakes under the same rule. The randomized order is optimal: on an explicit hard distribution under the minimal-prefix rule, every learner has expected mistakes at least $((T+1-\varepsilon)\,128^{-\mathbb{E}[Q]}-1)/2$, so $Ω(\log T)$ expected calls are necessary for polylogarithmic mistakes. The separation is governed by the oracle's selection rule, not by the class alone: for a legal feasible-median ERM rule a deterministic learner achieves $O(\log T)$ calls and mistakes, while a global-median rule again forces linear total cost. The same linear bound holds when the oracle's answers are chosen adversarially and then frozen into a memoryless oracle. We add partial tradeoff results for fixed query budgets (the middle regime is open) and an interface contrast: with only a weak consistency oracle, returning a realizability bit, both deterministic and randomized learners need $Θ(T)$ calls.

</details>


### [266] [Robust Beam Prediction for V2X Networks with Multi-Modal Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10200)
*Chen Shang,Dinh Thai Hoang,Diep N. Nguyen,Jiadong Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 面向V2X网络提出多模态波束预测框架BeamTransFuser，采用层次化Transformer逐步融合相机、LiDAR、雷达与GPS观测，并引入生成式模块重建缺失模态特征，在真实数据集上优于基线并提升不完整感知下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有感知辅助波束成形方法过度依赖射频感知，在复杂车载环境中可能不可靠；同时相机、LiDAR等异构传感器日益普及，为通过更丰富的环境感知提升波束预测提供了新机遇。

Method: 提出BeamTransFuser层次化Transformer架构，渐进式融合相机、LiDAR、雷达和GPS观测以进行鲁棒波束预测；并设计生成式模块，从可用观测中重建缺失模态特征，以应对实际部署中的模态缺失问题。

Result: 在真实多模态V2X数据集上的实验表明，所提框架持续优于代表性基线，生成式模块进一步提升了不完整感知条件下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 多模态融合与缺失模态重建可有效增强V2X波束预测的精度与鲁棒性，为ISAC-enabled V2X网络提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) provides a promising foundation for beam prediction in future vehicle-to-everything (V2X) networks. However, existing sensing-assisted beamforming methods still rely heavily on radio-frequency sensing, which may become unreliable in complex vehicular environments. Meanwhile, the growing availability of heterogeneous sensors, such as cameras and LiDAR, offers new opportunities to improve beam prediction through richer environmental perception. Motivated by this, this paper proposes a multi-modal beam prediction framework for V2X networks. Specifically, we develop BeamTransFuser, a hierarchical Transformer-based architecture that progressively fuses camera, LiDAR, radar, and GPS observations for robust beam prediction. In addition, to handle possible missing modalities in practical deployment, we introduce a generative module that reconstructs missing modality features from the available observations. Experimental results on a real-world multi-modal V2X dataset show that the proposed framework consistently outperforms representative baselines, while the generative module further improves robustness under incomplete sensing conditions.

</details>


### [267] [Hierarchical and Permutation-Invariant Feature Transformation Learning via Policy-Guided Embedding Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10225)
*Rui Liu,Tao Zhe,Yanyong Huang,Sankha Narayan Guria,Xiao Luo,Wei Fan,Yanjie Fu,Dongjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PHER框架，用置换不变的分层嵌入与策略引导多目标强化学习来提升表格数据的特征变换搜索，克服现有生成式方法的三类缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式特征变换方法存在三个关键局限：忽略低层特征、操作与高层抽象之间的层次关系；对本质置换不变的变换序列施加顺序敏感嵌入，引入系统性偏差；依赖梯度搜索，不适用于非凸变换空间。

Method: 框架包含两个互补组件：一是置换不变的分层模块，捕捉特征、操作与抽象层级间的交互，并用自注意力池化将语义等价结构映射为一致嵌入，且与下游性能对齐；二是策略引导的多目标强化学习搜索策略，从经验上较强的种子初始化，并联合优化预测精度与变换效率。

Result: 在多个表格数据基准上的大量实验表明，该框架相对强基线具有有效性和鲁棒性；代码与数据已公开。

Conclusion: PHER通过置换不变层次嵌入与策略引导多目标强化学习，缓解了层次关系缺失、顺序偏差与梯度搜索不适配的问题，提升了表格特征变换的预测性能与搜索效率。

Abstract: Feature transformation improves predictive performance on tabular data by constructing informative abstractions from raw features. Recent generative approaches encode transformation knowledge into continuous embedding spaces for efficient exploration of candidate strategies, but face three key limitations: (1) overlooking hierarchical relationships between low-level features, operations, and high-level abstractions; (2) enforcing order-sensitive embeddings on inherently permutation-invariant transformation sequences, thereby introducing systematic bias; and (3) relying on gradient-based search, which is ill-suited to non-convex transformation spaces. We propose a framework with two complementary components. First, a permutation-invariant hierarchical module captures interactions across features, operations, and abstraction levels, with a self-attention pooling mechanism that maps semantically equivalent structures to consistent embeddings aligned with downstream performance. Second, a policy-guided multi-objective reinforcement learning strategy initializes the search from empirically strong seeds and jointly optimizes predictive accuracy and transformation efficiency. Extensive experiments on diverse tabular benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of our framework against strong baselines. Our code and data are publicly available at: https://github.com/RayLiu1103/PHER.

</details>


### [268] [Training Trajectories Determine Circuit Removability in Annealable Soft-Prior Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10287)
*Zonglin Yang,Ziming Zhao,Wei Tang,Xunyu Jiang,Yihong Liu,Tailin Chen,Zifu Yu,Jiayu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究检验了软位置先验在学习检索电路后能否被移除而电路仍保持功能，发现平滑退火至零可保留性能，而强制置零或事后干预均失败，说明电路可移除性依赖训练轨迹而非最终架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软位置先验虽有助于小型Transformer学习检索电路，但当先验被移除后这些电路是否仍能正常工作尚不明确，需要厘清先验依赖与电路固化的关系。

Method: 使用注意力偏置可学习、淡出或置零的可退火软先验Transformer，在关联回忆与马尔可夫归纳任务上比较非强制、平滑淡出至零、强制置零、硬切换及事后继续训练等策略，并进行机制追踪分析。

Result: 关联回忆中先验激活时非强制模型表现良好（0.772±0.020），零门时崩溃（0.095±0.009）；平滑淡出至零保留高零门准确率（0.734±0.028），而强制置零、硬切换与事后继续训练均无法恢复同等效果。马尔可夫归纳呈现相同模式；线性回归ICL因零门训练可直接学会该任务而成为边界案例。机制追踪显示门降至零后发生电路固化，但负责头在不同随机种子间存在差异。

Conclusion: 在小型离散检索任务中，电路可移除性取决于训练轨迹而非最终架构，平滑的逐步退火对实现先验独立的功能性电路至关重要。

Abstract: Soft positional priors can help small Transformers learn retrieval circuits, but it is unclear whether the resulting circuits remain functional once the prior is removed. We test this with an annealable soft-prior Transformer whose attention biases can be learned, faded, or zeroed during training and evaluation. On associative recall, unforced models perform well with the prior active ($0.772 \pm 0.020$) but collapse at zero gate ($0.095 \pm 0.009$). Smooth fade-to-zero training preserves high zero-gate accuracy ($0.734 \pm 0.028$), whereas forced-zero training, hard switching, and post hoc continuation fail to recover the same effect. The pattern also appears on Markov induction. Linear regression ICL provides a boundary case because zero-gate training can learn that task directly. Mechanistic traces show that circuit consolidation occurs after the gate reaches zero, even though the responsible heads vary across seeds. These results suggest that circuit removability in small discrete retrieval tasks depends on the training trajectory, not just the final architecture.

</details>


### [269] [A Dominant Diffuse Phase in the Sparse Autoencoder Phase Diagram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10299)
*Alexis D. Plascencia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are increasingly used to recover interpretable features from neural-network activations, yet systematic feature co-occurrence can cause distinct features to be absorbed or merged. The MAIS-O43 open problem proposes a controlled experiment to characterize when recovery of a true synthetic dictionary gives way to feature merging as the nesting fraction $γ$, sparsity penalty $λ$, and dictionary size $M$ vary. We implement the specified protocol and evaluate 200 independently initialized fits across ten of the 165 grid cells. We observe zero full-dictionary recoveries and zero merges. Instead, every run converges to a reproducible diffuse phase: reconstruction is nearly perfect, but learned atoms typically remain far from the true features (median best cosine 0.5-0.7 against a 0.95 recovery criterion) and learned codes are an order of magnitude denser than the ground truth. This behavior persists under robustness checks and across the full 165-cell grid using standard minibatch Adam (3,300 additional fits). Since the global optimum of the exact sparse-coding objective is known to merge nested features in the two-feature case, these results suggest that trained SAEs need not reach the corresponding minima, and that the phase diagram of trained models may differ fundamentally from that of objective minimizers.

</details>


### [270] [View-Structured Conformal Prediction for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10307)
*Junzheng Chu,Bin Pan,Zhenwei Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出View-Structured Conformal Prediction (VSCP)，将新视角合成视为结构化回归，通过把预校准尺度分解为空间形状与可迁移的视角难度因子，实现有限样本有效的视角级覆盖预测，在13个真实场景中显著优于像素池化校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)虽能实时渲染新视角，但现有不确定性热图无法保证渲染视角达到特定的预测覆盖率。作者希望以结构化回归的视角，为每个新视角提供具有统计保证(概率至少1-α下覆盖至少1-β像素比例)的RGB预测盒。

Method: 提出VSCP方法：将预校准尺度拆分为来自渲染器的空间形状与可迁移的视角难度因子，该因子预测形状所需的最小视角级乘数。对视角做留出分位数(View-CP)以获得有限样本有效性，即使在迁移到新场景时也成立。该分解使分析精确：一致性分数为oracle视角难度与预测视角难度之比，超额宽度分解为测试侧与校准侧两项。

Result: 在13个真实场景中，像素池化校准达到89.9%边际像素覆盖但视角事件覆盖仅61.4%(目标90%)，而View-CP达到91.7–92.0%。在相同覆盖下，VSCP相比常数尺度将宽度缩减22.1%，并以每场景单模型、每查询4次光栅化(而非10次)匹配十模型集成21.0%的缩减。VSCP较最接近的单模型基线3DGS-U场提升4.7个百分点(p=0.0225)。视角预测器从有界源族迁移到全部九个无界Mip-NeRF 360场景，完整尺度在九个场景上均优于常数尺度，宽度节省20.7%；在不同稠密化骨干下仍保持18.3%节省，在RTX 4090上以216–280 FPS运行。

Conclusion: VSCP通过结构化分解与视角级共形校准，为3DGS新视角合成提供了具有有限样本有效性保证的覆盖预测，在宽度、效率与跨场景迁移性上均优于现有方法，且计算开销低、实时性良好。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) renders novel views in real time, but an uncertainty heatmap does not certify that a rendered view meets a certain prediction coverage. We treat novel-view synthesis as structured regression and ask that, with probability at least $1-α$, RGB prediction boxes cover at least a $1-β$ fraction of pixels in a new view. We propose View-Structured Conformal Prediction (VSCP). It splits the pre-calibration scale into a spatial shape from the renderer and a transferable view-difficulty factor, which predicts the smallest view-wise multiplier that shape needs. A held-out quantile over views (View-CP) then gives finite-sample validity even when transferring to new scenes. The same factorization makes the analysis exact: a conformity score is the ratio of oracle to predicted view difficulty, and excess width separates into a test-side and a calibration-side term. Across 13 real scenes, pixel-pooled calibration reaches 89.9\% marginal pixel coverage but only 61.4\% view-event coverage at a 90\% target, while View-CP reaches 91.7--92.0\%. At matched coverage VSCP cuts width by 22.1\% against a constant scale, and matches a ten-model ensemble's 21.0\% reduction using only one model per scene and four rather than ten rasterization passes per query. VSCP also improves on the closest single-model baseline, the 3DGS-U field, by 4.7 points ($p=0.0225$). The view predictor transfers from bounded source families to all nine unbounded Mip-NeRF~360 scenes. There the full scale beats the constant scale with 20.7\% width saving on all nine scenes. It also keeps an 18.3\% saving under a different densification backbone and runs at 216--280 FPS on an RTX~4090.

</details>


### [271] [One Loop, Two Gains: Can Active Learning win the Lottery for Free?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10311)
*Benedikt Tscheschner,Eduardo Veas,Marc Masana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将迭代幅度剪枝融入主动学习的重训练循环，提出 Improve & Prune (I&P)，在几乎零额外开销下于每轮主动学习中获得稀疏可部署模型，在高达 95% 稀疏度下匹配稠密模型精度，从而同时缓解每轮重训练与未标注池采集评分的计算瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 彩票假设的迭代幅度剪枝与深度主动学习都依赖"迭代重训练"，计算开销大，但二者长期被分开研究。作者观察到：基于池的主动学习迭代训练循环本身就提供了迭代幅度剪枝所需的计算结构，因此有机会将剪枝免费嵌入主动学习流程，并借此探究非平稳数据（主动学习）条件下能否产生中奖彩票。

Method: 提出 Improve & Prune (I&P)：在每个主动学习重训练周期同步执行幅度剪枝，几乎不增加额外成本；在多种采集函数、多种架构族、多个图像分类数据集以及主动微调场景下进行实证研究，考察非平稳数据 regime 下迭代幅度剪枝能否得到中奖彩票。

Result: I&P 在每个主动学习迭代都能产出稀疏、可部署的模型，在稀疏度高达 95% 时精度匹配其稠密对应模型，实质上把中奖彩票作为主动学习流程的副产品获得。

Conclusion: 主动学习的迭代重训练结构可被迭代幅度剪枝直接复用，二者结合能在无额外成本下同时解决当前阻碍深度主动学习在大架构与大未标注池上实际应用的两个计算瓶颈：每轮模型重训练与未标注池上的采集评分。

Abstract: The lottery ticket hypothesis posits the existence of winning tickets: sparse subnetworks that, when trained in isolation from their original initialization, match the accuracy of the full dense network. The predominant method for discovering such tickets, iterative magnitude pruning, alternates pruning with full retraining from scratch until convergence over many cycles. Similarly, deep active learning also retrains a model from scratch after each acquisition round as new labels become available. Despite this shared reliance on iterative retraining with a substantial computational overhead, the two paradigms have been studied separately. We observe that the iterative training loop inherent to pool-based active learning already provides the exact computational structure that iterative magnitude pruning exploits, and propose Improve & Prune (I&P), a method that integrates magnitude pruning into each active learning retraining cycle at practically no additional cost. This raises a key empirical question: can iterative magnitude pruning produce winning tickets under the non-stationary data regime of active learning? We investigate this question across multiple acquisition functions, architecture families, and image classification datasets, including an active fine-tuning scenario. Our results demonstrate that I&P yields sparse, deployable models at each active learning iteration. Those match the accuracy of their dense counterparts at sparsities up to 95%, effectively obtaining winning tickets as a byproduct of the active learning pipeline. These per-iteration sparse models can address two computational bottlenecks - per-round model retraining and acquisition scoring over the unlabeled pool - that currently prevent the practical adoption of DAL on large architectures and large unlabeled pools.

</details>


### [272] [A Later Test Set Is Not a New Domain: Pretraining Familiarity Survives a Contamination-Free Hold-Out](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10357)
*Mahdi Naser Moghadasi,Faezeh Ghaderi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个时间上完全后置于所有模型发布时间的数据集（13个预测模型、7个领域组、5个领域），发现预训练模型并非普遍占优，且其优势可由"语料熟悉度"而非季节性强度或谱熵等内在属性解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型几乎只在早于其发布的公开数据集上评估，高分无法与其在预训练中见过测试集区分开。作者希望用发布时间晚于所有模型的留出集来隔离记忆效应，并进一步探究预训练模型胜负的成因。

Method: 构建13个预测器（4个经典方法、3个按数据集训练、6个预训练）在来自5个领域、7个分组的任务上进行评估；所有观测均在最后一个模型发布之后，且所有数据集无需API密钥即可重建。随后检验输入窗口上测得的两项内在属性（季节性强度、谱熵）是否解释胜负模式，并分析语料熟悉度（以TimesFM作者自述的预训练语料主体Wikipedia pageviews为线索），在预训练模型族内部比较TimesFM族与Chronos族的排名。

Result: 在时间留出协议下，预训练模型7组中胜5组，1组败给Theta基线，在每日汇率上与季节性朴素预测及其它所有方法无法区分。季节性强度与谱熵无法解释胜负，季节性强度甚至与优势呈负相关。最大增益（周度Wikipedia pageviews上MASE较最佳经典方法低28%）恰落在TimesFM预训练语料主体所在领域，且粒度相同、仅时间窗口不同。预训练族内部，TimesFM族在Wikipedia上排名优于Chronos族0.53个名次，其它领域仅0.09（1,500 vs 754条序列，Mann-Whitney p < 1e-5）。

Conclusion: 时间留出只能消除对某一时间窗口的记忆，无法消除对某一领域的熟悉度；因此基准测试需要相对于已披露语料设定领域留出；对实践者而言，关键问题不是哪个模型更好，而是其所在领域是否为该模型"从小熟悉"的领域。

Abstract: Time-series foundation models are evaluated almost exclusively on public archives that predate them, so a strong score cannot be separated from having seen the test set during pretraining. The obvious remedy is a hold-out that postdates the models. We build one: thirteen forecasters -- four classical, three trained per dataset, six pretrained -- on seven groups drawn from five domains, every observation published after the last model was released, and every dataset rebuildable without an API key. Under this protocol pretrained models win 5 of 7 groups, lose one to a Theta baseline, and on daily exchange rates are indistinguishable from a seasonal naive forecast, along with every other method tested.
  We then ask what separates the wins from the losses, and report a negative result: the two intrinsic properties one would reach for -- seasonal strength and spectral entropy, measured on the input window -- do not account for the pattern, and seasonal strength is if anything negatively associated with the advantage. What does track it is corpus familiarity. Our largest gain (28% lower MASE than the best classical method, on weekly Wikipedia pageviews) falls on Wikipedia pageviews, the domain TimesFM's authors describe as the bulk of its pretraining corpus, at the same granularities and differing only in time window. Within the pretrained family, where every model forecasts identical series so that series difficulty cancels, the TimesFM family outranks the Chronos family by -0.53 ranks on Wikipedia against -0.09 everywhere else (1,500 vs. 754 series, Mann-Whitney p < 1e-5). We conclude that a temporal hold-out removes memorisation of a window but not familiarity with a domain, that benchmarks therefore need domain hold-outs stated relative to disclosed corpora, and that the practitioner's question is less which model is better than whether their domain is one the model was raised on.

</details>


### [273] [OmniMed-FL: A Robust Multimodal Federated Learning Framework for Clinical Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10364)
*Ayush Debnath,Ruelia Saha,Sudip Misra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 OmniMed-FL 可控系统研究，在多医院非IID联邦环境下对胸片与合成文本进行五分类多模态融合基准测试，仅作描述性代理比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床诊断常需同时评估医学影像与患者记录，但标准机器学习无法统一分析这两类数据；同时 HIPAA 与 GDPR 合规限制敏感数据集中聚合，导致跨远距离网络的安全视觉-文本融合存在空白。

Method: 代理语料由 3,000 张公开胸片与 3,000 条按类别（非按患者）匹配的合成笔记组成；在 Dirichlet 非IID 划分、3–20 家医院客户端下，基准测试 8 种融合策略、3 种初始化、4 种缺失文本插补规则及匹配的联邦基线。

Result: K=5、α=0.1 时 macro-F1：仅本地 0.297，FedAvg 0.662±0.074，FedProx 0.737±0.085，FedMME 式单次集成 0.647±0.080，SCAFFOLD-AdamW 适配 0.070±0.015，FedProx-FedAvg 差距 0.075 落在两倍种子标准差内。4×3 网格中标签偏斜损失至多 0.27 F1，客户端增至近七倍损失至多 0.10，K=20 时双向流量线性增至 183.5 GiB。多模态融合在合成语料上 0.956（文本 0.934、图像 0.664），在胸片语料上 0.906（0.880、0.737），模型状态为纯文本的 2.3 倍。

Conclusion: 多模态融合在两类语料上均优于单模态，但受合成笔记与非患者级配对限制，结果仅为描述性代理比较，不能视为诊断性能或部署就绪度估计。

Abstract: Simultaneous assessment of medical imaging and patient records is often required in clinical diagnosis. However, standard machine learning algorithms cannot analyze these data types together. Meanwhile, compliance with HIPAA and GDPR can constrain centralized aggregation of sensitive patient data.
